# Owner decisions log

**Purpose.** Read this when a reference doc, a discrepancy row or a plan number could have been
overridden by the product owner. Decisions arrive through `INBOX.md`; once implemented they are
recorded here (newest batch first) and the affected docs are updated or linked. Precedence:
ADRs > this log > the reference docs > the source transcriptions.

## Batch 2026-11-27 (the lane flow field's polyline agreement bound)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-27, given at about 06:58Z in the question dialog of session
eternalus-160 (the PR #98 merge session that continues eternalus-159). The owner's words are kept
verbatim outside the repository in `eternalus-agent-work/map-stack-2026-11-27/owner-answer-dec391.md`.
After DEC-227 (Q-A) made the nav cells 2.5 m, the `LaneFlowFields.test.ts` test "agrees with the lane
polyline" (from PR #82) measured a gap of 2.13% on the 5-lane match grid and failed its 2% bound.
SamarjitBhogal, who merged phase/3 into PR #98, left the red test to the PR #98 author (progress
entry 2026-11-26-2330). DEC-391 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Docs changed                                                 |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| DEC-391 | **On the 5-lane match grid, the lane flow field's progress and the lane polyline must agree within 2.5%, not 2%** (follows DEC-227 (Q-A); PR #98). The owner chose "A: allow 2.5%". In `packages/core/src/map/LaneFlowFields.test.ts`, the 5-lane match grid case uses `FIVE_LANE_MATCH_GRID_AGREEMENT` (0.025), and its comment cites this row. The other three cases (3 and 5 lanes on the bare grid, 3 lanes on the match grid) keep `POLYLINE_AGREEMENT` (0.02), because they measure 2% or less at 2.5 m cells (1.61% for 3 lanes on the match grid); the critic of PR #98 asked to loosen only the case that needs it. Minion strength reads this progress (DEC-280), so at the worst point a minion counts as about 2.1% less far along its lane than the polyline says. Measured on the 5-lane match grid with the map objects stamped (seed 1): 1.95% at 5 m cells (phase/3 before PR #98) and 2.13% at 2.5 m cells (lane-1, light team, near (603.9, 290)). The cell size alone causes the change: the phase/3 code at 2.5 m cells gives the same 2.13%. Options not chosen: (B) 2.2%, the same small margin as the old 2% over 1.95%; (C) keep 2% and change the flow field or the stamping, which changes how units path. | None; the bound is a test value, and no design doc states it |

## Batch 2026-11-26 (minion M6c: the seven interpretations)

The acting owner's (SamarjitBhogal) answer of 2026-11-26 to `INBOX.md` "Minion M6c
interpretations" (a) to (g), given in the PR #173 review session. DEC-376 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-376 | **The seven M6c interpretations are approved as built** (closes `INBOX.md` "Minion M6c interpretations"; PR #173). (a) balancing.md's checkpoint share counts the clearing team's living Legends within 10 m of where the set's last defender fell. (b) `totalDamage` is the damage those Legends dealt to the set, so the shares add up to the checkpoint's XP; when they dealt none, the XP is split evenly; nobody is paid when no Legend is in range or the killer is unknown. (c) The clearing team is the team of whoever killed the last defender. (d) The corner boss does not level (`canLevel: false` on `corner-camp`; DEC-260 names forest-camp mobs). (e) A guard that levels also gets and gives the `rallied` aura (DEC-165). (f) A set whose last waiting defenders are cancelled after its last living one died is never announced cleared and pays no checkpoint XP. (g) A set reached on the pass that changes the owner is the old owner's (DEC-257): its defenders that find room appear, and the cap-blocked ones are cancelled at once. | architecture 07 §7 |

## Batch 2026-11-26 (camp rewards: the clearing Legend; the three critic refactors)

The acting owner's (SamarjitBhogal) answers of 2026-11-26 to `INBOX.md` "Camp rewards" and
"`feat/camp-rewards` critic (2026-11-22), better than the design", given in the PR #153 review
session. DEC-375 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-375 | **The camp clear bonus goes to the Legend that killed the camp's last mob, as built; the critic's three refactors are dropped** (closes `INBOX.md` "Camp rewards" and "`feat/camp-rewards` critic (2026-11-22), better than the design"; PR #153). (1) Asked who "the clearing Legends" of balancing.md "Camps" are (as built, or (a) the last killer's whole team, (b) that team near the camp, (c) every Legend that damaged a camp mob), the acting owner chose **as built**: the one Legend that killed the camp's last mob gets `XP_SOURCES.campCleared`; when a minion or turret lands that hit, nobody gets it. (2) The three better-than-design items, (B1) split `ProgressionSystem`'s awards by recipient instead of by kind, (B2) a `Record<XpCause, rule>` in `LevelMatchesXpInvariant`, (B3) one `GuardStore` query for "this home still holds a guard", are **dropped**: the code stays as built, and the per-kind award branches are accepted. | architecture 05 §18; minion plan §3 (stand-in row removed) |

## Batch 2026-11-26 (the bot path cache's "one leg late")

The acting owner's (SamarjitBhogal) answer of 2026-11-26 to `INBOX.md` "Bot path cache (PR #84)",
given in the PR #84 review session. DEC-373 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-373 | **The bot path cache is accepted as built: a way that opens is found at most one leg late** (closes `INBOX.md` "Bot path cache (PR #84)"). Asked "(a) accept as built; (b) store the grid's version with the cache and search again when it changes", the acting owner chose (a). The cache holds no grid version: at runtime the grid only opens cells, so a bot never walks into a closed cell, and a way that opens while it walks a cached leg is found when it reaches that waypoint. If a runtime change ever closes cells, store the grid's version with the cache (option (b)). | architecture 11 §6 |

## Batch 2026-11-23 (Owner Calls page: the Cathedral gem's open rules)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-23 on the Owner Calls page to the five Cathedral gem cards of PR #132
(`feat/cathedral-gem-hittable`), posted on that pull request in swholtzman's re-review R6. DEC-346 is
this batch, the number the re-review assigned. Built in `feat/cathedral-gem-hittable` on
2026-11-23.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-346 | **The Cathedral gem blocks movement and no longer strikes by touch; a death by it counts for the victim's last enemy damager; both "tick" readings stay; the two tidy-ups are built; the Duel design is a follow-up** (closes `INBOX.md` "The Cathedral gem: four rules DEC-292 leaves open" and the two better-than-design items "split the Cathedral interior builder" and "one shared 'never damaged' structure entry"; DISC-11; architecture 07 §1). The owner's words, verbatim: **GEM duel:** "Other (note) — I don't know how big a change this may be, but it could be a cool idea for the damage and stun to behave differently. Rather than simply applying the damage and stun to the one who attacked it, perhaps it applies them in a circle around it. This way, a magus or zealot could shoot projectiles at it from afar and use it as a damaging thing. However, if one legend shoots it X times in N seconds, it fires a laser at the damaging individual if they are out of range. The laser does massive damage and stops on contact with the targeted legend. It does not damage objects or buildings and is blocked by them. This offers a neat experience in both 10v10 and duel, where it can be used as cover so that the attacker can shoot it and apply damage and stun to the enemy nearby, but also to themselves if they're too close. Meanwhile, if the targeted legend ducks behind it and goads their attacker into hitting it, they may take damage from the stun and damage, but then the attacker gets lazered." **GEM middle lane:** "Other (note) — The gem blocks movement but the battle happens around it. Walking by it doesnt activate the damage." **GEM kill credit:** "Other (note) — same time-out as damage done in kills or assists. last one to damage the dying legend as long as the damage was dealt within N seconds. if the kill comes directly from another entity using it as a weapon against an enemy, they get the kill (in battlefield 6, if you destroy a stucture and it falls on an enemy and kills them, you get a kill and its icon is a falling brick. same idea). If an entity hasnt taken damage from an enemy in N seconds and they die to the gem, they get the death added to their stats but no one gets a kill or xp." **GEM tidy-ups:** "Yes to both, inside #132 — sorry, what about the forest camps? why can they never break?" **GEM tick:** "Keep both readings (as built): burn 1.2 s, 6 hits, 72 damage; glow about 1.17 s". What each changes: (1) **Middle lane.** The touch strike and its `gem-warded` ward are gone; only a hit with an attacker strikes. The gem blocks movement: an unbreakable plinth (a `cathedral` map object, which has no health) the size of the gem's body stands under it (`CathedralInteriorBuilder`), so collision and the lane paths go round it. (2) **Kill credit.** A death by the gem's statuses names the victim's last enemy damager (any unit of another team: a Legend, a minion, a tower) whose hit landed within the assist window (`SCORING.assistWindowSeconds`, 30 s) as its killer; the source kind and id stay the gem's burn (the "falling brick"). One place decides it (`KillCredit`, called by `DamagePipeline` before `entity-died`), so scoring, XP, veterancy and the invariants agree; only a Legend killer on another team earns a Legend kill, as before. With no enemy hit in the window the victim takes a death and nobody a kill or XP. The last enemy hit is match state (`LastEnemyHit`, hashed). The owner's other case, a kill "directly from another entity using it as a weapon", needs the Duel follow-up's circle strike and is built there. (3) **Tick.** Both readings stay as built: the burn lasts 1.2 s and hits 6 times for 72; the glow lasts `gem-shock` + 5 game ticks (about 1.17 s). No change. (4) **Tidy-ups.** The Cathedral furnishing moves to its own `CathedralInteriorBuilder` (the built map objects are identical for every seed checked); camps and the gem share one `NEVER_DAMAGED` spread in `structureCatalogue.ts`. The owner's question on why forest camps never break is a new `INBOX.md` item. (5) **Duel.** The gem stays in the Duel arena, and the gem system stays off in a Duel until a follow-up PR builds the circle strike and the laser; that needs numbers the owner has not given (six cards "GEM duel (1)" to "(6)" on the Owner Calls page). This replaces the DEC-292 parts "any touch" and "a death by it is nobody's kill"; the DEC-292 row stands as history. Docs changed: architecture 07 (§1 gem row, implementation status), 05 §9 (kill credit at the damage pipeline), `INBOX.md`. |

## Batch 2026-11-23 (Owner Calls page: the zone level-up clean-ups)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-23 on the Owner Calls page to `INBOX.md` "Minion M10
better-than-design items" (a) to (c), relayed on PR #131 by swholtzman's reviewer session.
DEC-353 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Docs changed                                                                       |
| ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-353 | **The zone level-up's three clean-ups are built in PR #131 before it merges** (closes `INBOX.md` "Minion M10 better-than-design items"; removes 03 §4's one exception, so the round-2 S2 sign-off request is no longer needed). Asked "Zone level-up: build the three code clean-ups now, or keep the code as built?", the owner wrote: "Build all three in #131 before it merges". (a) A `MinionLevelSystem` after `ZoneLevelUpSystem` owns every minion level change: `VeterancySystem` announces kill levels by event (`minion-level-earned`) too, so no listener changes state in its handler. (b) `StatusEffects.removeStacks(id, n)` goes through the applier, so a lost level sends one event (`status-stacks-lost`, or `status-expired` when no stack stays), not a `status-expired` plus one `status-applied` per remaining level. (c) The hold time and the number of levels are fields of each capture-point `ZoneDefinition` (`levelUp`), found by id (balancing.md's knob table), not one global constant; DEC-241 stays: the hold time becomes ticks at load. | architecture 03 §4 and its event catalogue, 05 §8, 07 §7, 18 implementation status |

## Batch 2026-11-23 (Owner Calls page: checkpoint defender follow-ups)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-23 on the Owner Calls page to `INBOX.md` "Minion M6b follow-ups"
(4), (6) and (7), relayed on PR #130 by swholtzman's reviewer session. DEC-350 is this batch
(other numbers from DEC-317 on are other sessions').

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Docs changed                                              |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-350 | **Checkpoint defenders: a fixed limit per capture point, late defenders only for the current owner, and the capture point boss group levels** (closes `INBOX.md` "Minion M6b follow-ups" (4)'s boss-group question, (6) and (7); PR #130). (7) "A fixed maximum number of defenders for each point (give the number in the note) — You choose what makes the most sense in terms of numbers.": the reviewer chose **10** defenders per capture point, one full defence (checkpoint 1: 4 medium; checkpoint 2: 2 heavy + 4 light, `CHECKPOINT_DEFENDERS`), so the first push always gets its full set and a contested point never builds a crowd. The 10 counts every living defender at the point, of any side, plus the team defenders waiting for room under the unit cap; when a reached checkpoint's set does not fit, only the defenders that fit appear, in table order, and the rest never appear. The number lives in content next to `CHECKPOINT_DEFENDERS`, citing DEC-350; review it if checkpoint 3's massive mob is added. (4) "Yes, the mini-boss and its support mobs level up": the capture point's boss group levels like neutral defenders and forest-camp mobs (DEC-260). (6) "Other (note) — Defender appears later, given that the team still controls the point. If the point changes hands, the defender is cancelled.": a team defender the unit cap blocks waits and appears when there is room; when the point changes owner, every defender still waiting for the old owner is cancelled; a waiting defender counts toward the limit of 10 until it appears or is cancelled. Built in M6c (`feat/minion-m6c-defender-followups`), as the minion plan places it. | `INBOX.md` "Minion M6b follow-ups"; minion plan §1.2 rows |

## Batch 2026-11-23 (Owner Calls page: automatic reconnect in a Duel)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-23, on the Owner Calls page, to question Q1 of the 2026-11-22 review
of PR #104 ("Should the game reconnect a dropped Duel player on its own?"), relayed in the
2026-11-23 re-review of PR #104, which reserved DEC-349 for it. The owner's words are quoted.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Docs changed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-349 | **A dropped Duel player reconnects on its own** (PR #104, SCRUM-430; changes DEC-297, which left automatic rejoin as an unscheduled follow-up). "Build automatic reconnect now, before this work merges: the player sees "Reconnecting..." and gets back in with no click". When the connection drops once the player is paired, `DuelSession` enters a `reconnecting` state, the HUD shows "Reconnecting..." (`ReconnectingNotice`, through the bridge's `duel-connection` event), and the session retries with back-off, presenting the seat's guest id, until the server gives the seat back (`match-found` again, which goes on with the same Duel: no new VERSUS screen and no new loading) or the reconnect window ends; only then is the Duel lost (DEC-297 (1), DEC-250 (4)). FR-118 (ratings in a database) stays in sprint 4 (DEC-297 (3)). Defaults taken in the build, not the owner's words: the back-off waits (`RECONNECT_BACKOFF_MS`, `// TUNE:`, a network pacing choice); the client counts the window from the moment it sees the drop, with the server's value (`RECONNECT_WINDOW_MS`, now shared through `@eternalus/protocol`); a drop while still queued goes home as before; an attempt the server queues instead of seating (it has not seen the drop yet) leaves the queue and tries again. | `apps/client/src/game/duel/DuelSession.ts`, `DuelSession.types.ts`, `DuelSession.constants.ts`; `game/SessionController.ts`; `bridge/events.ts` (`duel-connection`), `bridge/slices/matchSlice.ts`, `bridge/connectBusToStore.ts`; `ui/hud/ReconnectingNotice.tsx`, `ui/screens/MatchScreen.tsx`, `ui/strings.ts`; `packages/protocol/src/presence.constants.ts` (`RECONNECT_WINDOW_MS`); `multiplayer-and-scalability-plan.md` §7 implementation status and question 44; `INBOX.md` SCRUM-430; decisions DEC-297 |

## Batch 2026-11-22 (Owner Calls page: the Duel test time limits)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-22 on the Owner Calls page (relayed by session
PR-REVIEW-COORDINATION-15) to two questions from session eternalus-141: after DEC-227's smaller
nav cells, two Duel tests on PRs #98 and #122 went over their time limits on the ubuntu-latest CI
runner. The owner's words are kept verbatim outside the repository in
`eternalus-agent-work/owner-calls-2026-11-20/answers-v37/answers/` (`server-test-timeout.json`,
`core-duel-test-timeout.json`). DEC-325 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Docs changed                                                                                           |
| ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| DEC-325 | **The server's tests get the same 60 s time limit as core's and client's, and the core Duel kill-target test gets its own 120 s.** (1) The owner chose: "Give the server the same 60 s limit as core and client, on #98". `apps/server/vitest.config.ts` sets `testTimeout` to 60 s, as `packages/core/vitest.config.ts` and `apps/client/vitest.config.ts` do; the server had Vitest's 5 s default. `CoreDuelSimulationFactory.test.ts`, "builds a Duel with one distinct Legend per seat that advances", took 5.13 s on ubuntu-latest (PR #98, run 44864054893, head `aa01127f`) and 1.38 s on phase/3 before DEC-227. (2) The owner chose: "Give this test its own 120 s limit, as DEC-289 did, and widen DEC-289's INBOX item "Make the tower horde test faster" to cover both slow whole-match tests". `Simulation.duel.test.ts`, "ends the match for the first team to reach the kill target", passes its own time limit, `KILL_TARGET_TIMEOUT_MS` (120 s); every other core test keeps 60 s. It took 61.7 s on ubuntu-latest and 26.2 s on windows-latest (PR #122, run 44872612846, head `ce0ce423`) and 38 s on the same code at head `56bcd64b`: runner speed under coverage, not a regression. The cause of both: DEC-227's 2.5 m nav cells (4 times the cells) make a whole-match build about 3 times slower; a CPU profile puts the time in `WorldMap.stampFootprint`, `cellsInside` and `LaneFlowFields`. The cost: a run of either test that really hangs fails later (after 60 s, not 5 s; after 120 s, not 60 s). | ID      | Decision | DEC-291 | **The Cathedral gem keeps its size and diamond shape, and shows the colour of the team holding the Cathedral** (closes `INBOX.md` "The Cathedral gem's look"; DISC-11; architecture 07 §1). Asked "Keep the gem's size 10x16, colour #9fd3e3 and diamond shape?", the owner picked "Change size or colour (say which)", with the note "the blue gems are for the light team. A blood-red-purple version is for the dark team. the capture point, neutrals, etc are purple-violet." So the size (10 × 16) and the diamond stay; the colour follows the Cathedral's holder: blue for Light, blood-red-purple for Dark, purple-violet while neutral. The owner gave no hex values; the defaults taken in the build are Light `#4f9fe0`, Dark `#8c1c4a`, neutral `#8a5cd0` (`apps/client/src/game/views/CathedralGemView.constants.ts`, `TUNE`). Because the colour follows match state, the gem is drawn by a client view from its own snapshot (`CathedralGemView`), no longer as static map scenery.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | architecture 07 §1, §13; `CathedralGemView` (client); `INBOX.md`                                                           |
| DEC-292 | **The Cathedral gem has its own hittable, unbreakable body, and hitting or touching it stuns and burns the hitter** (answers `INBOX.md` "The gem's lightning on attackers: 11 open rules" and the gem-body question; DISC-11; architecture 07 §1). Asked "Is the gem the Cathedral's only body, with hit radius 5?", the owner picked "Give the gem its own hittable body now, apart from the building", with the note "the cathedral gem is "hittable" but cannot be destroyed. attacking it hurts any entity. neutrals, bots, etc do not intentionally target it. if they make contact by accident, it applies a toned-down stun and tick-damage. same goes for anyone including the player. projectiles and abilities also apply. tick damage hits for the duration of the stun + 1 tick. can kill if player health is low. you choose damage. should be a reasonable amount of damage to deter attacking it on purpose, but not a game-breaking amount. crystal lets out a purple "pulse" aura when hit and glows purple for the stun duration + 5 ticks. multiple hits make it glow brighter and the aura of the glow grows." Built as: a `cathedral-gem` structure at the Cathedral feature (hit circle the diamond's half-width, `never` breakable), blocking no movement; any blocked hit on it with an attacker (weapon now; a shot or ability aimed at the gem lands through the same damage pipeline) and any touch land `gem-shock`, `gem-burn` and `gem-warded` on that unit as environment damage, so they hurt neutrals too (`CathedralGemSystem`). The player can aim an attack at it (pick order: a unit, then the gem, then a map object; amends DEC-150 / DEC-183). Defaults taken in the build (`TUNE`): stun 1 s (half the 2 s default `stun`); burn 12 every 0.2 s for 1.2 s, so 6 hits and 72 in all (the note's "stun + 1 tick" read as one burn pulse of 0.2 s; read as one game tick it would be 5 hits and 60) (about an eighth of a Warrior's health), able to kill, and a death by it is nobody's kill; glow `gem-shock` + 5 ticks (read as 5 game ticks; what "tick" means in the note is an open Owner Calls question); a touch strikes a unit at most once every 5 s (`gem-warded`), because a lane runs through the Cathedral's centre: a minion that walks across the gem takes one strike; a unit that stays on the gem is struck every 5 s and can still die. Off in a Duel for now (DEC-36: the world round the arena is switched off) until the owner answers `INBOX.md` "The Cathedral gem: three rules DEC-292 leaves open". | architecture 07 §1 and implementation status; 03 §4, §5; 04 §1; 18 §3; `structureCatalogue.ts`, status effects; `INBOX.md` |

(1) "(c) CSS typed arithmetic (critic r2)", no note: the overlay's numeral leaves the select artboard (`SelectionStage`) and the HUD frame sizes it. `.match` sets `--stage-scale` to `calc(var(--hud-u) / 1px)`, the HUD frame's scale as a number, and enlarges the 64 px select numeral by `calc(var(--stage-scale) * 216 / 64)`, so its glow still grows with it; the veil's grid centres it again. `SelectionStage` and its window resize listener leave the HUD, so the HUD has one fit source, `--hud-u` (architecture 13 §7), with no exception. (2) In chat on 2026-11-20, to "keep the @supports fallback for browsers without typed arithmetic (Safari < 26, Firefox < 158)": "sure keep it". Under `@supports not (scale: calc(1px / 1px))` the numeral takes the same 216 HUD units as its font size (the HUD's floored form) and no `scale`, so its glow stays at the select size there; the development Mac's Safari is 18.6. Default taken in the build, not the owner's words: `--stage-scale` is set on `.match` itself, so the select numeral's font floor (`max(64px, calc(var(--text-min) / var(--stage-scale)))`) reads it unchanged and `motionTokens.test.ts` needs no third font-size form (the card expected one). | `apps/client/src/ui/components/Countdown.module.css`, `Countdown.tsx`, `SelectionDefs.constants.ts`, `SelectionStage.tsx`, `useStageScale.ts`; `ui/hud/MatchCountdown.tsx` and `MatchCountdown.module.css`; architecture 13 §3; reference 15 CD-49; `INBOX.md` HC-B15; decisions DEC-299 |

## Batch 2026-11-20 (the countdown critic's better-than-design items)

The architecture critic's round 2 on `feat/match-start-countdown` found two better-than-design
items (BTD2, BTD3; DEC-139), and its round 3 two more (BTD5, BTD6). No plan schedules them, so they
are built in that branch under the owner's standing rule for such items (2026-11-09, DEC-223 and
DEC-232): "do not allow tech debt where we can eliminate it" and "no tech-debt if possible, when
possible". DEC-286 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Affects                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| DEC-286 | **A UI enhancement id that is typed but not listed fails the type check, the urgent countdown glow is the countdown glow at another alpha, the timeline frame is built in one place, and screen-reader lines have one path** (BTD2, BTD3, BTD6, BTD5). (1) `UI_ENHANCEMENT_IDS` keeps its literal ids (`as const satisfies readonly UiEnhancementId[]`), and its test asserts with `expectTypeOf` that they are every `UiEnhancementId`, so a new member of the type that the list misses fails `tsc` (the pattern of `assetVocabulary.test.ts`). (2) The urgent glow def spreads the countdown glow def and maps its shadows to 30 %, so a change to the countdown glow's region, σ or offsets edits one place. (3) One `config/timelineFrameOf(config)` builds the `TimelineFrame` that `MatchTimeline.resolve` and `validateGameConfig` resolve marks against, and `Simulation` hands its `countdownEndTick` to `GameState` in a named `GameStateTiming` (`schedule`, `countdownEndTick`), so `state/` no longer imports a value from `config/` (architecture 02 §3: `state/` may take `config/` types only; critic round 3 m7, which this closes). A `GameState` built without it, as in most unit tests, has no countdown (0), as it has no schedule. (4) Architecture 13 §7 states the one-live-region rule: every line for a screen reader goes into `LiveRegion` through `useLiveRegion`, and no component makes a live region of its own (DEC-282 (7)). | `apps/client/src/game/content/screenPresentation.ts` and its test, `apps/client/src/ui/components/SelectionDefs.constants.ts`; `packages/core/src/config/timelineFrameOf.ts`, `config/validateGameConfig.ts`, `match/MatchTimeline.ts`, `Simulation.ts`, `state/GameState.ts` and `GameState.types.ts`; architecture 13 §7; decisions DEC-282, DEC-285 |

(1) "From countdown end": the timeline's phase marks take their fractions of the planned match from the countdown's end, so DEC-81's "mid-game is at 15 minutes" is 15:00 on the match clock, like DEC-87's wave clocks; each mark fires one countdown later in real time than before. (2) "Core MatchResult": core records the match time as `MatchResult.matchTicks`, the ticks from the countdown's end (0 when the match ends during the countdown); the results screen's match length reads it, and `durationTicks` stays the replay fact. (3) "Share + UE-01 pulse now": the match-start overlay, the character select and the win/lose screen draw one countdown component; UE-01 (ui-enhancements-plan §3: a tick each second from 10 s, the larger pulse and the urgent glow from 5 s, a screen-reader line at 10 s and at 5 s) replaces DEC-278's strike-in on the overlay and is on for the character select. (4) "Sign off, update §4.2": the bridge derives the overlay's countdown from `MatchSnapshot.countdownEndTick`, as built; game-loop-and-results-plan §4.2 says so. (6) "Keep it static": UE-01's urgency is only where a player must act before 0, the character select and the match start, so the win/lose screen's countdown stays the Figma's static numeral. (7) "Fix it": the screen-reader line's live region is in the page, empty, before its line, so a countdown that appears at 10 s (the match-start overlay) still says "10 seconds left" as a change. Defaults taken in the build, not the owner's words: START takes UE-01's `final` look (the larger pulse and the urgent glow, no line); the line is one shared string, `STRINGS.countdownAlert`, not the plan's `STRINGS.characterSelect.countdownAlert`; the thresholds live in `ui/components/Countdown.constants.ts` (presentation, not gameplay); (7) is built as one polite region at the app root (`ui/components/LiveRegion.tsx`), mounted by `App` outside the screen switch, into which each countdown portals its line (critic round 2, M1: a region of the countdown's own, filled after the render, still received its line in the same task); an empty `ui=` developer flag turns every enhancement off, and unknown ids are dropped; test content turns every enhancement off (`TEST_CONTENT.uiEnhancements`), so the earlier tests keep the Figma original. ## Batch 2026-11-19 (chat and question dialog: the match-start countdown)

The owner's words of 2026-11-19, relayed in chat by the PR review coordinator: "during the initial
10-second hold, the system counts DOWN from 10 with dramatic effect, not starting the timer at 0
and counting UP to 10. initial 10 seconds do not count toward the game time; this is simply to
allow the game to catch up with the spawning and let players have some suspense before the game
starts. the screen should be covered by a tinted overlay with the countdown in the middle in red
numbering with the same details and effects as the pre-game match countdown." The scope answer of
the same day, in the question dialog: "Both, same look". Both are kept verbatim outside the
repository in `eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-words-2026-11-19-countdown.md`. DEC-278
is this batch (DEC-276, DEC-277, DEC-279 and DEC-280 are other sessions').

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Affects                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-278 | **The match starts with a countdown over the whole screen, and the countdown is not match time** (amends DEC-81's HUD clock and DEC-129 (1); keeps DEC-88; DEC-87). (1) The local match's 10 s hold counts down from 10 in the middle of the screen, over a tinted veil, in red numerals. (2) "Both, same look": the Duel keeps its 3 s countdown and draws its 3, 2, 1 and START with the same overlay. (3) The countdown is not game time: the HUD's match clock starts at 00:00 when the countdown ends, and the results screen's match length leaves it out. Core's `MatchResult.durationTicks` stays the ticks the match ran, a replay fact (a build choice, signed off as DEC-282 (2)). (4) So that the clock and the countdown hold after the `countdown-over` mark has fired (a Duel joined late), core's `MatchSnapshot` carries the countdown's end tick, from config (a build choice, signed off as DEC-282 (4)). Defaults taken in the build, not the owner's words: "the pre-game match countdown" is read as the character select's countdown (DEC-60's "pre-game menu"), so its colour, Cinzel Bold and glow; the veil tint (`--selection-ink-veil`) and the numeral size; each number struck in (the select's countdown is static, so this is the build's reading of "with dramatic effect"; this default is replaced by DEC-282 (3)); a local match shows no START (its veil goes when the countdown ends); START shows for the Duel's first second after the countdown, over the same veil; the numbers do not move under `prefers-reduced-motion`; the overlay takes no pointer events, so the player can turn during the hold (DEC-88). | `packages/core/src/state/Snapshot.types.ts`, `state/GameState.ts`; `apps/client/src/game/MatchBridge.ts`; `bridge/events.ts` (`MatchClock`); `ui/hud/MatchCountdown.tsx`, `ui/hud/DuelHud.tsx`; architecture 03 §5, 13 §3 and §5; decisions DEC-87, DEC-88, DEC-129 |

## Batch 2026-11-19 (chat: harness fixes)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-19 in chat, to the offer to end the `docs/plan/PROGRESS.md` merge
conflicts. The options offered: (a) one file per progress entry, (b) leave it. The offer was item C
of the chat, so the owner's answer "C: fix them" picks (a). DEC-277 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-277 | **Each progress entry is its own file in `docs/plan/progress/`.** Owner: "C: fix them". Built option (a): one file per entry, named `YYYY-MM-DD-HHMM-<branch-slug>.md`, so two sessions never edit the same file. Before this, every session appended to the end of one 8,600-line `docs/plan/PROGRESS.md`, and each PR merged into `phase/3-sprint-3-work` made every other open PR conflict on it. `PROGRESS.md` stays as the archive of the earlier entries, unedited except its intro. The session-start hook and the live instructions (`AGENTS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, the skills, the agents, `docs/agents/`) point to the folder. Rejected: (b) leave it. Built on `chore/progress-entry-files`. The generated `docs/README.md` index lists only `docs/plan/progress/README.md`, not each entry file, so the index is no new conflict point; the docs check still validates every entry file (owner 2026-11-19, "Skip entries"). The owner waived the ADR that `docs/process/guardrails.md` §4 asks for when a guardrail changes, because the binding gates (pre-commit, pre-push, CI) do not change (2026-11-19, "Waiver in DEC rows"; DEC-254 is the precedent). Better than the design, owner approved 2026-11-19: a check that fails when an existing entry file is edited (`INBOX.md` HC-B11). Transition (owner 2026-11-20, Owner Calls card "DEC-277 transition"): "Cut over at the merge: entries a branch wrote before it merged a phase/3 that has DEC-277 stay in PROGRESS.md; every entry after that is a file in progress/". |

## Batch 2026-11-19 (chat: harness fixes)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-19, in chat, to the stop hook timing out under parallel load.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Docs changed                                                                                         |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-276 | **The Stop hook checks only the files this working tree changed.** With 5 to 11 sessions running ESLint at once, `npm run check:quick` took about 292 s and the 290 s timeout killed it, so the hook printed "Checks failing" with no error although a direct run exited 0. Options offered: raise the timeout, lint only changed files, leave it. Owner's pick: "B: fix it" (lint only changed files). Built: `scripts/hooks/stop-check.mjs` skips when no lintable source changed; runs the full `check:quick` when `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `tsconfig*.json` or `eslint.config.js` changed; else runs the no-JS check, `tsc -b` and ESLint on the changed files. A deleted source file still triggers the typecheck, because its importers can break; ESLint gets only the files that exist. A timeout still exits 2 but says it is a timeout, not a check failure. `run()` in `scripts/hooks/hookInput.mjs` kills the command's whole process tree on a timeout, and when the hook itself gets SIGINT, SIGTERM or SIGHUP: before, it killed only `npm`, and its ESLint child kept running with nobody reading it (18 such orphans on 2026-11-19), which loaded the machine and made the next hook time out too. Why this option: a longer timeout only moves the cliff and every idle session still pays it. Cost: a lint finding in a file you did not touch is found by the git hooks and CI, not by this hook; `tsc -b` and the no-JS check still cover the whole repo. The owner waived the ADR that `docs/process/guardrails.md` §4 asks for when a guardrail changes, because the binding gates (pre-commit, pre-push, CI) do not change (2026-11-19, "Waiver in DEC rows"; DEC-254 is the precedent). Better than the design, owner approved 2026-11-19: run the hook tests in `npm run check` (`INBOX.md` HC-B10). | `docs/architecture/16-agentic-harness.md`, `docs/process/guardrails.md`, `docs/agents/tool-setup.md` |
