| DEC-256 | **The feature-keyed groups are part of the snapshot contract** (GL-16 critic round 5, better-than-design item; amends DEC-255; DEC-139). `FeatureKeyedGroup` is declared in `state/Snapshot.types.ts` beside `EntitySnapshot.mapFeatureId` (`keyof Pick<EntitySnapshot, 'health' \| 'capture'>`), not in the invariant's companion file, so the client depends on the snapshot contract, not on a diagnostic. The invariant's `FEATURE_GROUPS` stays in `MapFeatureGroupsUniqueInvariant.constants.ts` (which component backs each group is the invariant's detail) and `satisfies Record<FeatureKeyedGroup, unknown>`, so a missing or an extra group does not compile. Owner: "Type in snapshot (Recommended)", noting "do whichever YOU assess is better"; the session kept that option over the critic's (move `FEATURE_GROUPS` into `state/` too), which would put in `state/` a runtime constant nothing there uses. It takes a sixth critic pass. | `refactor/map-feature-link`: architecture 03 §5, 13 §10, 15 §3; game-loop plan §7 (GL-16); `INBOX.md` HC-Q60 |

## Batch 2026-11-14 (chat: the CI job time limit)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-14, in the question dialog (session 3ef229eb), to the open item of
the PR #98 split work: the time limit of the CI job. The owner's words are kept verbatim outside
the repository in `eternalus-agent-work/tower-ruins-and-routes/owner-answer-2026-11-14-ci-timeout.md`.
DEC-254 is this batch (DEC-250 to DEC-253 are other sessions').

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| DEC-254 | **The CI job may run for 25 minutes, not 15.** The `check` job in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` has `timeout-minutes: 25`. On one commit of PR #98 (`16c7fe2d`, run 43646563122) the windows-latest job was cancelled at the 15-minute limit, and its re-run passed in 9.5 minutes: runner variance, not a slow test. The cost: a job that really hangs fails 10 minutes later. Owner's pick: "Raise to 25 (Recommended)", over 20 and over leaving 15 and re-running each cancelled job. Built in its own PR (scope `ci`). |

## Batch 2026-11-14 (chat: the online Duel in the results flow, INBOX HC-Q57)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-14, in chat (session GAME-LOOP-AND-POST-GAME-30 and its successor), to

shown as two options on a decision page: "agreed. option b is better. also leaving a duel when the

another session's, PR #96).

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Affects                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-250 | **The Duel's results use one ending type, one rematch rule and one view model; a finished Duel gets the run-on later; leaving a Duel is a loss** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q57; DEC-139). (1) A critic (`architecture-reviewer`) passed HC-Q57 (1) to (4), so they are built on PR #93: `selectMatchVerdict` gives no verdict for a Duel the other player left, and `selectResultsView` holds the results screen's rules; the session publishes the Duel's setup when the first snapshot arrives, before `match-loading`, so the verdict's team always comes from the setup; `MatchEnding` in `@eternalus/protocol` is the one type for the wire message, the session and the bus event; `canRepeatSetup` in `bridge/matchSetupRules.ts` is the one rematch rule (only a local match's setup repeats), read by the session and the screen. The same critic kept `duel-ended` as the event's name, kept the `scoreBook` field under DEC-210 (the read-only type for all the `MatchRecord` writers, built with HC-S7), and kept a finished Duel without a status line (verdict lines are content keyed by victory condition, and the server sends none). (2) HC-Q57 (5), the match-mode strategy, is scheduled with the HC-S7 split. (3) HC-Q57 (6): option B. A finished Duel gets the run-on of DEC-92: the server keeps stepping and sending the world for the delay after the end, refuses commands, then closes; the client keeps the connection open and uses the local match's delay path; the delay value gets one home that server and client read. It is not on PR #93: it is scheduled with the Duel's own win rules (DEC-36). Until then a finished Duel goes to the results at once, as a scheduled interim. A Duel the other player left goes to the win/lose screen at once in both versions. (4) New rule: a player who leaves a Duel while the other player is still in it takes a loss. Nothing records a Duel's result for the player who left yet, so this is built with the Duel's own win rules. What the player who stayed sees (a victory, or the plain "Opponent left.") is not decided; until it is, that screen shows no verdict. | `packages/protocol/src/messages.types.ts`; `apps/client/src/bridge/matchSetupRules.ts`, `bridge/selectors/selectResultsView.ts`, `bridge/selectors/selectMatchVerdict.ts`; `apps/client/src/game/SessionController.ts`; architecture 13 §2, §3, §5, §10 and 02; `INBOX.md` HC-Q57, HC-S7, HC-Q46; decisions DEC-36, DEC-92, DEC-139, DEC-210 |

## Batch 2026-11-14 (question dialog: the DEC-239 generator rules)

A teammate's review of PR #98 called the DEC-239 row a blocker, because it says the owner was not
asked. The owner first asked for a critique of the four generator rules (2026-11-13: "critique
these to see if they are within the design, or as-good-or-better-than the design"). The critique
found all four within the design (DEC-215 (1), DEC-227, DEC-235 (B5)) and found that none
overwrites a better mechanism on phase/3 or on the open branches
(`eternalus-agent-work/tower-ruins-and-routes/critic-dec239-rules-2026-11-13.md`). The owner then
answered in the question dialog (2026-11-14; kept in
`eternalus-agent-work/tower-ruins-and-routes/OWNER-ANSWERS-2026-11-14.md`). DEC-246 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-246 | **The four DEC-239 generator rules are the owner's rulings: "Confirm"** (the question: "Do you confirm them as owner rulings?"). (1) The map generator refuses a map where two lane tower forts overlap, checked on the whole map (`assertLaneFortsApart` in `map/laneStructures.ts`). (2) Towers of one kind on a lane stand at least a fort box's diagonal apart, `hypot(width, height)`; towers of different kinds on one lane only must not overlap. (3) A lane's tower band widens to hold two or more towers of a kind, and a tower that would leave the lane is refused. (4) A navigation cell as thick as the thinnest fort wall, or thicker, is refused when the grid is built (`map/MatchGrid.ts`). DEC-239's row stands as written; this row adds the owner's confirmation. The critique's one nit, the diagonal as `max(width, height) * sqrt(2)`, was fixed before this answer (`fa7808ce`). |

## Batch 2026-11-11 (question dialog: where seconds become ticks)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-11 to one question from the review of PR #85 and PR #79 (session
PR-REVIEW-ASSIST-23). The reviewer saw that architecture 11 said durations become ticks "where they
are read", and architecture 03 says "at load". The owner chose the option "as built, fix on own
branch": sign off the built conversion for #85 and #79, and build the load-time conversion for all
five readers on a new branch straight away. DEC-241 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Changed                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-241 | **Content durations become ticks once, at load** (confirms architecture 03 §8, "durations are ticks converted from seconds at load"). Content states a duration in seconds. A loader turns it into ticks once with the match's tick rate, and systems and controllers read only ticks. This now holds for the six readers that converted at read time: the leash grace and the aggro cooldown (`loadAggroRules`), the stuck window (`loadStuckDetection`), the veterancy aura period (`loadMinionVeterancy`), the spawn waves (`loadSpawnWaves`), the guard respawn time (`loadGuardPlans`) and the out-of-combat healing delay and structure quiet time (`loadOutOfCombatHealing`; #86 merged after the owner's answer and added this sixth reader, which the rule covers; signed off by DEC-262). The same healing loader also turns the healing rates from per second into per tick, so that system sees only ticks, as `loadDefaultContent` already does for speed and acceleration (architecture 03 §10: systems only ever see ticks; pre-PR critic, better than the design, signed off by DEC-262). No tick value changes. A time of 0 s stays 0 ticks where 0 has a meaning (a guard's leash grace and cooldown, DEC-144); `TickRate.roundToTicks` does this, and `TickRate.toTicks` keeps its floor of one tick. The wave timing rules are checked in the loader, for every authored wave. The other five loaders reject a time that is negative or not finite (`assertNonNegativeFinite`; pre-PR critic, better than the design, signed off by DEC-262), so every loaded duration is a valid tick count; the healing loader rejects such a rate too. | `refactor/durations-at-load`: the six loaders and their tests, `TickRate`, `LeashBehaviour`, `StuckSystem`, `VeterancySystem`, `SpawnSystem`, `GuardRoster`, `OutOfCombatHealingSystem`, architecture 04 and 11. |

## Batch 2026-11-10 (question dialog: bot A\* search cap on 2.5 m cells)

A finding outside the critic list on `feat/tower-ruins-and-routes`: DEC-227 (Q-A) made the nav
cells 2.5 m, and the A\* search cap stayed at its 5 m value. The owner chose option A in the
question dialog. Evidence:
`eternalus-agent-work/tower-ruins-and-routes/astar-finding-2026-11-10.md`. DEC-240 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-240 | **One A\* search may settle 24000 cells** (follows DEC-227 (Q-A); `GameConfig.map.pathMaxExpansions` in `config/defaultGameConfig.ts`, was 6000). On 2.5 m cells, 6000 cells cover a quarter of the area they covered on 5 m cells. With a horde at every lane tower, bot Legends' searches failed 190 to 273 times in 300 ticks (seeds 1, 2, 10), all on routes of about 470 m, and a failed search stops the bot and drops its target. 24000 covers the area that 6000 covered on 5 m cells and clears every failure in that test; one failed search costs about 36 ms at most. A bot path cache (re-plan only when the goal moves or the grid changes, the next step architecture 11 §6 names) is an `INBOX.md` request for its own branch. |

2026-11-11, "Product owner decisions (2026-11-10)"), which reserved DEC-236 and DEC-237 for that

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Changes                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-237 | **Boss kill rewards.** The owner's words: "killing mini-bosses can go up 2 levels at most (new decision). Killing the middle boss puts you up 5 levels from wherever you are, flat (for the time being until a better idea comes into play)." And for the corner boss: "team-wide is fine for now." So a mini-boss kill (the outer capture points' and the corner camps') pays team-wide and lifts no Legend more than 2 levels; the Eternal's kill lifts each rewarded Legend exactly 5 levels. Built for the capture point boss on `feat/capture-point-boss`: its `balancing.md` 500 XP goes to every Legend of the killer's team, each paid at most what brings it 2 levels up (`XP_SOURCES.teamKills`); the killer gets nothing on its own. | `packages/core/src/content/progression/xpSources.ts` (`teamKills`, `TUNE`), `ProgressionSystem`, `level-matches-xp`; `05-abilities-weapons-and-loadouts.md` §13 status; `INBOX.md` capture point mini-boss interim (d) |

## Batch 2026-11-10 (question dialog: the tower-ruins critic's better-than-design items)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-10 to two of the five better-than-design items from the first
critic round on `feat/tower-ruins-and-routes` (DEC-227), given through the question dialog. His
words are kept verbatim outside the repository in
`eternalus-agent-work/tower-ruins-and-routes/OWNER-ANSWERS-critic-r1-2026-11-10.md`. He was not
asked about B3 to B5: his standing rule of 2026-11-09 ("do not allow tech debt where we can
eliminate it and always choose the option with the best architectural outcome") applies, so they
are built too. DEC-235 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-235 | **Tower-ruins critic items B1 to B5 are built** (amends DEC-217 (12) and (15), DEC-227). (B1) "Could both work together? ... choose the better design for architecture and performance": a mob in a court leaves by `AStarPathfinder.exitWaypoint`, whose answer for each blocked cell is searched once and kept until the grid changes (`WorldMap.version`), an exit field filled on demand. The answer is the same as a search every tick. (B2) "Move rule out of store": `WorldObjectStore` keeps only object identity and destroyed state; the ruin rule is `StructureRuins`, run by `MapDestructionSystem`'s listeners, which also reopen the grid. The struck wall is still the one nearest the killer's position. (B3) `MatchWorld.build` is the one place that decides whether a feature is stamped as court ground or as a footprint, and `MatchWorld.reopen` undoes it. (B4) A\* has one search loop for a goal and for a way out of a court. (B5) `map/fortInWorld.ts` gives a lane tower's fort in world units, used by the tower spacing, the capture-point zones and a build check that refuses nav cells as thick as the thinnest fort wall. |

## Batch 2026-11-09 (PR #85 review: a chaser inside an outline holds)

A teammate's change request on PR #85 (2026-11-09): "Minions already inside a building's attackable
outline keep walking toward its boundary, although combat considers them in reach." The fix and
the critic's better-than-design item on it are built under the owner's standing rule for such
items (2026-11-09, GL-15 critic calls, DEC-223): "choose the best design and the most
future-proof option with the least tech debt" and "do not allow tech debt where we can eliminate
it". DEC-234 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Where it lands                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-234 | **A unit that stands inside its target's outline is in reach and holds there; one aim rule says so** (follows DEC-185 (E), DEC-219 (d) and 18 §5.2, a chase stops at reach; DEC-220 (1) "reach and chase still measure to the outline edge" is read as "to the outline, not the centre", a reading the owner confirmed through the question dialog on 2026-11-10: "Yes, hold inside"). `EntityStore.aimPointOf` gives the attacker's own position when it stands inside the outline, so reach (`isInReach`), the minion and guard chase (`PathingSystem`) and stuck detection (`StuckSystem`) read one point, and a mob inside the Stronghold's palace no longer walks out to its wall while it attacks. Better than the design (critic): `aimPointOf` returns what it aimed at as a tag (`AimedAt`: `'outline'`, `'inside'`, `'centre'` or `null`), so the inside test runs only there; `isInReach` drops its own, and `facingPointOf` (PR #79, DEC-220 (1)) reads `'inside'`. | `fix/minion-stack-followups` (PR #85): `EntityStore`, `PathingSystem.chase.test.ts`, 18 §5; merged into `feat/out-of-combat-heal` (#86) and `feat/unit-facing-chevron` (#79), where `facingPointOf` uses the tag. |

against it and take the better design". DEC-225 to DEC-227 are this batch (DEC-221 to DEC-224 and
DEC-229 belong to other sessions). Each row amends the rows it names.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-225 | **Out-of-combat heal: the built rules stay; the Stronghold's zone is only the ground inside its walls, counted by the centre point; heal tidy items (2) to (11) are approved** (amends DEC-188 and DEC-215; the heal questions Q-D to Q-H and the heal better-than-design items in `INBOX.md`). (Q-D) Only a hit that deals damage marks a structure as under attack; a blocked or zero-damage hit does not. (Q-E) The owner's words: "only the area inside the stronghold. doors/gates not included. must be inside the structure (at least half the entity, as other areas are also done)". The ground of the shield's walls, gates and turrets is outside the zone, so the band in front of a gate is outside. "As other areas are also done": the other zones, the heal fort boxes and the capture zones, count a unit by its centre point (DEC-215 (3)), and along a straight edge a unit whose centre point is inside has at least half its body inside. The centre-point rule stays for every zone, so one rule serves all of them; an exact half-the-body test would make the Stronghold count units differently from capture. No code change. (Q-F) A human whose hit on an unbreakable gate or Stronghold deals no damage stays out of combat. (Q-G) A hit on the palace map object is not a hit on the Stronghold itself. (Q-H) When a shield wall falls, its ground stays outside the zone: the zone is read once from the map. Better than the design (DEC-139), approved for the tidy PR (DEC-218): the heal items (2) to (10) as `INBOX.md` lists them, and (11) one fort geometry (question dialog, "One source"): the capture court of a capture-point fort comes from the box of that fort's drawn walls, made smaller by the wall thickness, so the heal and capture zones read one fort shape. The two zones stay different on purpose: a tower's heal box includes its walls and gateways (DEC-215 (4)), a capture zone is the court only (DEC-145 (a)). Q-D to Q-H are built on `feat/out-of-combat-heal` (PR #86). |
| DEC-226 | **A fort's court is not inside the building for facing; the chevron critic's items (2) to (4) are approved** (amends DEC-220 (1), as a confirmation). (Q-I) A fort's outline is its walls and its keep, so its court is outside every outline. A unit in the court that attacks the tower faces the spot where it deals damage (DEC-216 (11)), which can be a wall behind it, as built. Better than the design (DEC-139), approved for the tidy PR (DEC-218): (2) `isInReach` and `facingPointOf` share one private "attacker inside the outline" helper; (3) the target-geometry queries (`outlineOf`, `aimPointOf`, `isInReach`, `facingPointOf`) move out of `EntityStore` into their own query object; (4) `SpawnContextReader` checks the position with `isFiniteVector` too, and its last cast goes. Q-I is built on `feat/unit-facing-chevron` (PR #79).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| DEC-227 | **Tower ruins: 2.5 m navigation cells, ruined walls at half health, all 11 fort pieces are walls; the generator keeps forts apart** (amends DEC-217 and DEC-219). (Q-A) Units in a fort court find a route out through finer navigation cells: the cell size goes from 5 m to 2.5 m (DEC-217 (15)). (Q-B) A ruined wall piece has half the wall's own health, 2000 to 1000, as built. This agrees with DEC-219 (a): the wall that took the killing hit falls with the tower, and each other wall starts at half health. (Q-C) All 11 pieces of a fort are walls and become ruins: the 7 wall pieces and the 4 corner towers. (Question dialog, "Tower-ruins map PR") The map generator places towers of one kind in a lane at least one fort box apart, so two forts and their heal zones never overlap (DEC-215 (1)), also when `spawnTowersPerLane` or `buffTowersPerLane` is 2 or more. Build all of this on `feat/tower-ruins-and-routes`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## Batch 2026-11-09 (the owner's calls on the GL-15 pre-PR critic, round 1)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-09 to the questions of the first pre-PR architecture critic of
`feat/capture-scoring` (GL-15), given through the question dialog. The owner's words are kept
verbatim outside the repository, in `eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-11-08-prepr.md`,
section "GL-15 critic round 1 owner calls". DEC-223 is this batch.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Docs changed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-223 | **A scoring rule is a listener or a tick scorer; capture scoring is two rules; a team counts the checkpoints it reaches; the event names the Legends on the zone and the hold rule reads each point's owner; all built on GL-15** (GL-15 critic round 1, better-than-design items 1 to 5; DEC-139; refines the rule types of DEC-221 (2)). (1) A listener rule had to have a per-tick step (`scoreTick`), which was empty in `StructureDestroyedRule`, and it got the whole `GameState`. The agent recommended an optional `scoreTick`. Owner: "choose the best design and the most future-proof option with the least tech debt". The agent chose two roles: a `ScoringListener` gets only the event bus and buffers what an event earns through `defer` (so it writes the `ScoreBook` only at the fold, DEC-211); a `TickScorer` scores once per fold from the `ScoreBook` and the `GameState`. `ScoringRules` lists each role, and each rule implements only the role it uses. No method is optional. (2) `CaptureScoring` splits into `CheckpointRule` (a listener, DEC-214 (A1)) and `CaptureHoldRule` (a tick scorer, DEC-214 (A2)). Owner: "Split now on GL-15 (Recommended)". (3) `TeamStats` gains `checkpointsReached`, the number of checkpoints the team's pushes reached, so the results can show a team's score by source. Owner: "Add it now on GL-15 (Recommended)". (4) Asked to keep the two remaining items as built, to let the event carry the Legends on the zone (as a follow-up PR) or to count the owned points from the zones, the owner selected all three and wrote "do not allow tech debt where we can eliminate it and always choose the option with the best architectural outcome". So both changes are built now on GL-15, not in a follow-up (a follow-up is the debt the owner ruled out). (4a) `checkpoint-reached` carries `presentLegendIds`: the reaching team's living Legends whose centre is on the zone when the checkpoint is reached (DEC-145 (a)). `CaptureSystem` fills it in the pass that weighs the sides, and `CheckpointRule` gives those Legends `checkpointsWon`; it no longer scans the store for them. (4b) `CaptureHoldRule` reads each capture point's owner from its `Capturable` at each fold. The scoring state no longer keeps a count of owned points (`ownedPoints` is removed). | `feat/capture-scoring`: `match/ScoringRule.types.ts`, `match/Scoring.ts`, `match/defaultScoringRules.ts`, `match/rules/CheckpointRule.ts`, `match/rules/CaptureHoldRule.ts`, `match/rules/StructureDestroyedRule.ts`, `state/ScoreBook.ts`, `state/ScoringState.types.ts`, `state/GameEvents.types.ts`, `systems/CaptureSystem.ts`; architecture 02 §2 and §9, 03 §2 and §4, 07 §7, 12 (paths, §1, §5, §12, §13), 17 R3 |

## Batch 2026-11-09 (combat sprint: world health bars)

The owner's brief of 2026-11-09 that opened the combat sprint, and his answers the same day to
five questions given through the question dialog (sessions COMBAT-WEAPONS-ABILITIES-1 and -2).
DEC-195 is this batch; DEC-195 to DEC-209 are the abilities and combat work's block (see the batch
of 2026-11-05 evening).

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-195 | **A unit's world health bar is hidden until it fights, then shows until it dies; enemies red, allies green, never the player's own** (new). The owner's words: "have health bars show up on any enemy that has taken damage (when an entity has full health after spawning and hasn't yet taken damage, their health bar is hidden; when they engage in battle by attacking a living entity (not an object entity) their health bar appears and remains visible until death)." (1) A unit's bar is hidden from its spawn until it takes damage or deals damage to a living unit (a Legend, mob or boss). A hit on a map object or a structure does not count. From then on the bar shows until the unit dies, also after it heals back to full; a respawned unit starts hidden. (2) Scope: allied units show a bar by the same rule (answer "Allies too"). The player's own Legend shows none, because the HUD shows the player's health (answer "No, HUD only"). (3) Colour: every enemy bar, camps included, has one red fill (answer "One red for all"); every ally bar has one green fill (answer "Green for allies"). The exact colours and the bar's size are placeholders (`TUNE`). (4) Towers and the Stronghold get no world bar by this rule; structure bars come with the structure-views task (answer "Leave for later"). Built in `apps/client/src/game/HealthBarVisibility.ts` (the rule) and `game/views/HealthBarView.ts` (the drawing), the look in `game/views/HealthBarView.constants.ts` (`HEALTH_BAR_*`); architecture 08 §8. |

(3) The GL-5 critic proposed: B1, a `MapFeatureLink` component, set from a placement's map feature, that the snapshots read and that `MatchBridge` uses to bind a capture site to its zone by identity, not by position; B2, one helper that keeps the last published array while nothing in it changed, for both HUD rosters (structures and capture points); B3, a bridge-local `opponentOf`. Owner: "B2 on GL-5 now (Recommended), B3 on GL-5 now (Recommended), B1 as its own follow-up PR (Recommended)". So B2 and B3 are built on `feat/results-flow`; B1 is plan task GL-16 and an `INBOX.md` item. | ID      | Decision | DEC-214 | **Capture and hold points are a task of their own, GL-15, built now on a branch stacked on GL-4; both are team-only points; the HUD Wall tile shows its gates' summed health** (builds DEC-131's capture and hold values; FR-007). (A) Asked whether to build the capture and hold points in GL-4 or in a separate PR right after it, the owner answered "make sure this happens. separate pr approved only if this item is assured to happen.": so GL-15 is a row in the game-loop plan §7, an `INBOX.md` request item, and a branch (`feat/capture-scoring`, base `feat/match-scoring`) that is built in the same session as GL-4's last critic round and opened as a PR after GL-4's. (A1) When a team's push takes a capture zone past checkpoint 1, 2 or 3 (`checkpoint-reached`), that team gets `SCORING_WEIGHTS.captureCheckpoint` (3, `TUNE`, DEC-131) as team-only points, as for a destroyed structure (DEC-212); its Legends in the zone get only the `checkpointsWon` counter (architecture 12 §5). Owner: "Team-only points (Recommended)". (A2) Each tick, every capture point a team owns adds one tick to its held time (`capturesHeldTicks`); the team gets `SCORING_WEIGHTS.holdPerMinute` (1, `TUNE`, DEC-131) team-only points for each full minute of that pooled time, which a lost point does not reset. Owner: "Pooled time, team-only (Recommended)". Neutral camp units can capture (DEC-145) but score nothing. (B) Wall segments are not structure entities yet, so their health is unknown. The HUD strip's one Wall tile (all of a team's wall segments and gates, DEC-108) sums the health of the members whose health is known, which today are the gates, instead of showing no data; it shows no data only while no member's health is known. A wall segment counts as soon as it becomes a structure. A destroyed member still marks the tile destroyed (DEC-108). Owner: "Show the gates' summed health" (the agent had recommended keeping "no data"). | `feat/match-scoring`: game-loop plan §7 (GL-15), `INBOX.md`, `content/match/scoring.ts` (the `ponytail:` note); `feat/capture-scoring`: `content/match/scoring.ts`, `match/Scoring.ts`, `state/ScoreBook.ts`, architecture 12 §5; `feat/results-flow`: `bridge/selectors/selectStructureStrip.ts` (Wall tile), architecture 13 §10 |

## Batch 2026-11-08 (the owner's calls on the GL-4 and GL-5 pre-PR critics, round 2)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-08 to the questions on the pre-PR architecture critics of
`feat/match-scoring` (GL-4) and `feat/results-flow` (GL-5), round 2, given through the question
dialog. His words are kept verbatim outside the repository, in
`eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-11-08-prepr.md`. DEC-211 to DEC-213 are this
batch (DEC-210 to DEC-214 are the game loop's).

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Changes                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-211 | **A `ScoreBook` with named writers keeps the scores; guard kills count as mob kills; the victim rules are a registry; the director takes scoring through an interface** (GL-4 critic round 2, findings 1 and 6, BTD-1 to BTD-3; adds the scoring writers to DEC-172's list). (1) Asked whether to make the public scoring state read-only (the agent's recommendation) or to move the scoring writes into a small class with named writers, the owner answered "i want whatever will be the best option for the future of the game" and left the call to the agent. The agent chose the class: structure points, captures, holds and boss kills each add a writer, and named writers keep the rule "a team's score is the sum of its Legends' points plus its team-only points" in one place. So a `state/` class, `ScoreBook`, holds the scoring data (plain data, hashed) and is its only writer (add a row, award points, record a hit, take the assists, award team-only points); `MatchRecord` holds it; `MatchState.scoring` is a deep read-only view; `Scoring` keeps only the rules (what counts, 12 §5). (2) Enemy tower guards are team mobs: a guard kill scores by its kind and counts in `minionKills` (owner: "Count guards"). (3) The victim's family picks its scoring rule through a registry, not an if/else, so team boss kills add one entry. (4) The director takes scoring through a one-method interface that `Scoring` implements, not `Pick<Scoring, 'fold'>`. Owner on (3) and (4): "Both now". | `feat/match-scoring`: `state/ScoreBook.ts`, `state/MatchRecord.ts`, `state/MatchState.types.ts`, `match/Scoring.ts`, `match/MatchDirector.types.ts`; architecture 03 §2, 12 §4 and §5                            |
| DEC-212 | **Destroying an enemy structure scores 5 team points, and the HUD structure strip shows the real structures** (GL-4 critic round 2, finding 2; builds DEC-131's structure value; FR-007). The team that destroys any enemy structure (the Stronghold, a spawner tower, a buff tower or a gate) gets `SCORING_WEIGHTS.structureDestroyed` (5, `TUNE`, DEC-131) as team-only points, not a Legend's, and its `structuresDestroyed` goes up by 1. As for structure XP (DEC-186), the opposing team scores, whoever lands the last hit. Neutral camps score nothing. Capture and hold points still wait for the capture work. The owner added: "also ensure the HUD structure views are wired-up to the real structures, please": the HUD structure strip takes each structure's health from its live entity, not `null`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `feat/match-scoring`: `content/match/scoring.ts`, `match/Scoring.ts`, `state/ScoringState.types.ts`, architecture 12 §5 and §13; `feat/results-flow`: `apps/client/src/game/MatchBridge.ts`, architecture 13 §10 |
| DEC-213 | **The results flow takes four better-than-design items** (GL-5 critic round 2, B1, B2, B3 and B5; DEC-139). (B1) `MatchBridge` takes the simulation's event subscription and keeps its own listener table, so each announcement 12 §8 plans adds one line. (B2) `createMatch` takes one match observer (`crashed`, `ended`) instead of one callback per event. (B3) The session countdowns are one record by kind, with one action and one selector. (B5) A test checks that every default victory condition has verdict lines. B4 (keep the `selection` assets loaded on a rematch) was not chosen: `INBOX.md` HC-S11.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `feat/results-flow`: `MatchBridge`, `Match`, `SessionController`, `matchSetupSlice`, `connectBusToStore`; architecture 12 §8, 13 §2, §5 and §10                                                                  |

## Batch 2026-11-06 (question dialog: facing inside an outline, camp mobs, guards at their posts)
