through the question dialog (session `2383bba1`). DEC-220 is this batch. The row amends the row it
names. **Correction to DEC-217 (14), same date:** the row said a unit is stuck when it "moves less
than 1 m in 2 s". The owner's answer was option (a), "stuck after 2 s without 1 m progress", which
is what the code measures. DEC-217 (14) now says that.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-220 | **A unit inside a building faces its centre; camp mobs face out from the camp centre; a guard back at its post faces outward again** (amends DEC-216). (1) A unit whose position is inside a building's outline faces the building's centre when it attacks it. Reach and chase still measure to the outline edge. DEC-216 (11) applies to a unit outside the outline. (2) A camp mob faces outward from the centre of its camp when it spawns, as built. DEC-216 (6) named only tower guards. (3) A guard that is back at its post after a chase or a leash return turns to face outward again (on guard). (4) The chevron critic's better-than-design item 1 (one start-facing port that `EntityFactory` uses, and a guard-plan field `facesOutward`) goes to the tidy PR (DEC-218), not to PR #79. Build (1) to (3) on `feat/unit-facing-chevron` (PR #79). |

## Batch 2026-11-05 night (question dialog: fort walls, refill counts, veteran and rallied, branch scope)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-05 to four questions, given through the question dialog (the
session that continued session `ccc465d4`). DEC-219 is this batch. The row amends the rows it names.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-219 | **The fort wall that took the killing hit falls with its tower; refill counters count attempts; veteran and rallied combine as built; DEC-217 is built on its own branch** (amends DEC-217, DEC-169 and DEC-164). (a) DEC-217 (12) is confirmed: the fort wall that took the hit that destroyed the tower falls with the tower and leaves a gap. A tower shares one health pool with its fort walls (DEC-217 (13)), so that wall is at 0 health. Each other wall of the fort starts at half health and blocks until it is destroyed. (b) The guard refill counters count attempts, as built: a refill that is blocked when it is due and filled later counts as 2 requested and 1 suppressed. (c) Veteran (additive) and Rallied (multiplicative) combine as built: a stat is (1 + the sum of the additive modifiers) × the product of the multiplicative modifiers. A rallied minion at veteran level 3 deals ×1.3 × 1.05 = ×1.365 damage, not ×1.35. (d) DEC-217 (12), (15) and (17) are built on a new branch after `fix/minion-stack-followups`, not on that branch. That branch builds the critics' should-fix list and two better-than-design items: the leash decision parts are built once, and one outline rule and one aim rule serve reach, chase and stuck detection. The critics' other better-than-design items wait in `INBOX.md` for the tidy PR (DEC-218). |

## Batch 2026-11-05 evening (decision page: heal, facing, tower access, better-than-design)

The owner's answers to 22 open calls, given on the "Eternalus Owner Calls" page on 2026-11-05 and
exported by him. His words are kept verbatim outside the repository in
`eternalus-agent-work/playtest-2026-11-04/OWNER-ANSWERS.md`, section "Round 3". DEC-215 to DEC-218
are this batch (DEC-195 to DEC-209 are the abilities session's, DEC-210 to DEC-214 the game loop's).
Each row amends the row it names; the code changes are to be built on the branch each row names.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| DEC-215 | **Heal zones never overlap; structure and Legend heals stack; the Stronghold heals only inside its walls** (amends DEC-188; questions 1 to 5). (1) Two structure heal zones never overlap: towers stand far apart, and so do Citadels. An overlap is a bug in the code, not a case for a rule. A structure's heal zone and a heal from an allied Legend (for example a Magus heal or a healing pool) stack. (2) The Stronghold is under attack when the Stronghold itself was hit in the last 10 seconds; hits on its walls, gates or turrets do not count. The Stronghold's heal zone is only the area inside its walls: when walls become mountable and Legends can control the turrets, a Legend must not heal while it controls a turret. (3) A unit is in a zone when its centre point is inside. (4) A tower's zone is a box round the fort walls, and the gateway counts. (5) The Stronghold turret tips past the shield arc are outside the zone. Build on `feat/out-of-combat-heal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| DEC-216 | **Units start facing the enemy Stronghold; guards face outward; a unit faces the spot it damages** (amends DEC-192; questions 6 to 11). (6) Before its first move or attack, a unit faces toward the enemy Stronghold. A tower guard faces outward from the centre of its tower (on guard). (7) A unit turns to its attack target while the target is in reach, also between hits; a stunned unit does not turn. (8) The chevron keeps its curved base arc. (9) The chevron is gold `#d9a93a` for every unit. (10) The disc and ring stay as built: the team colour is in the disc, and the ring is white (Legends) or near-black (mobs). This closes the ring-colour question of DEC-192. (11) A unit that attacks a building faces the spot where it deals damage, not the building's centre: its weapon (when weapons exist) acts on that spot. Build on `feat/unit-facing-chevron` (PR #79).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| DEC-217 | **A dead tower's fort becomes ruins with half-health walls; routes out of a court now; gate reach to the footprint; stuck-unit research** (amends DEC-185 and DEC-193; questions 12 to 17). (12) When a tower dies, its fort becomes ruins and scenery. Each fort wall then has half health, except the wall that was attacked to destroy the tower (owner's words: "the fort walls have half-health unless that wall was what was attacked to destroy the tower"; the reading that this wall falls with the tower is to be confirmed). Each wall blocks until it is destroyed on its own. (13) Only towers share one health pool with their fort; the Stronghold palace and the gates keep their own health. (14) A unit is stuck when it makes less than 1 m of progress toward where it heads in 2 s, and a waypoint is reached within 1 m: build this first. Then research how League of Legends and Smite 2 handle stuck units. The main reference is The Lord of the Rings: The Battle for Middle-earth; its code may be public through the modding community, and if so it can help with hordes, bots, large armies and camps. (15) Fix the routes out of a tower court now, not after the map work. (16) For the chase rule, the Stronghold palace box is the fort box. (17) Reach to a gate is measured to the gate's footprint, like towers, not to a 3 m circle. Build on `fix/minion-stack-followups`. |
| DEC-218 | **Better-than-design items B1 to B5 are accepted** (DEC-139; the critics' items from the click, minimap and chevron work). (B1) Target picking moves into a core `PlayerController` when that is built. (B2) A typed unit-family union, with `'unit'` in `EntityFamily`, comes with B1. (B3) The player-lookup helpers move out of `Match.ts`, (B4) one `opposingTeam` helper replaces the four copies in core, and (B5) an abstract `UnitView` base class draws the chevron for `LegendView` and `MobView`: B3 to B5 in one tidy PR after the playtest PRs merge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Changes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-190 | **XP rework: one kill or one structure must not raise a Legend by many levels** (the owner's request; this note is not final). The approved numbers: the level curve is `110 + 2 (L - 1)` XP for level L to L+1, which is 7,742 XP to the cap; a Legend kill pays `80 + 1.5 x victim level`; a structure destroyed pays 120 XP to every Legend of the team (a dead Legend: see DEC-186); minion XP does not change. The target: a Legend with 10 or more Legend kills, 100 or more minion kills and 4 destroyed towers at 20 minutes is around level 40. The model run gave level 39 for a horde mix and level 32 for mostly light minions; the worst single event gives 0.94 of a level (model results, as built). The owner's note: "approved but put a note that this isnt final and could change in the future if we find that levelling is not happening how we like." **This row supersedes the 300 XP value of DEC-186 (now 120), and in part DEC-90:** the target "level 50 at about 20 minutes" and its curve become the target and curve above. The rise of the XP step with each level (DEC-90) stays. Owner answers of 2026-11-05: a kill of an odd-level Legend pays a half XP, and core keeps it exact ("Keep exact, HUD rounds": the HUD shows whole numbers); the design test stays "at most 1 level per same-level kill or structure" ("Keep 'at most 1 level'"). | `feat/structure-xp`: `packages/core/src/content/progression/levelCurves.ts` and `xpSources.ts` (the numbers stay in `content/`, `TUNE`), `loadLevelCurves.test.ts`, `xpSources.test.ts`; `balancing.md` XP section and INBOX XP lines with the code PR  |

(3) The minimap uses one projection for drawing and for clicks.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `fix/click-attack-units` (1, 2): `apps/client/src/game/findUnitAt.ts`, `PlayerCommands.ts`, the core unit families list; `feat/local-minimap` (3): `engine/camera/MinimapProjection.ts`, `MiniMapView.ts`; architecture 06 §8 and §10 with the code PRs |
| DEC-192 | **Units show their facing with a small chevron** (a new request; the owner asked "if we are touching entities at all"). His reference image: a light-grey filled disc, a team-coloured ring (orange) with a dark gap, and a small gold open chevron (two lines on a curved base arc) just outside the ring at the front. Defaults, owner accepted 2026-11-05: the facing is the movement direction; a unit turns to its attack target while it attacks; it keeps its last facing when idle; the chevron is gold for every unit and the team shows in the ring; the chevron shows in the main view only, not on the minimap. Core has `Movement.heading`, but it goes to zero on a stop and is not in `EntitySnapshot`, so the facing needs a core change. Being built on `feat/unit-facing-chevron`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `feat/unit-facing-chevron`: the facing in core and `EntitySnapshot`, the views in `apps/client/src/game/views/`; architecture 04 and 06 with the code PR                                                                                                |
| DEC-193 | **A stuck unit paths around the obstacle, and the lane minion leash grows by 15 percent** (answer to the tower-access defaults, question E; amends DEC-185). A unit that is stuck does not shove against a wall for ever: it finds a path around to its target. The owner asked for "detection on being 'stuck'". On "increase leashing distance by 15%", the owner chose "Lane minions only" (2026-11-05): the lane minion leash grows by 15 percent and the call-for-help radius follows it (it is the leash, DEC-144 (D)); tower guard and camp mob leashes do not change. Default (not asked): stuck detection applies to minions (lane, guard, camp) and to bot Legends that chase a target; the player moves by input. Being built on `fix/minion-stack-followups`. ## Batch 2026-11-05 (the owner's call on the match director's critic round 3, `feat/match-director`)

The owner's answer of 2026-11-05 on one better-than-design finding of the match director's pre-PR
critic, round 3 (`INBOX.md` HC-Q46), asked in chat. The stored answer is kept verbatim outside the
repository, in `eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-11-05-hc-q46.md`. The owner
took the agent's recommendation.

| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-210 | **Only the match code gets the match writers: the public `GameState.match` is the read-only `MatchState`** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q46; narrows DEC-177, enforces DEC-172's "one caller each"). Asked: `GameState.match` is a `MatchRecord`, so every system, command handler and bot controller that holds `GameState` can call `endMatch`, `holdIdle` and the other writers, and only review keeps each writer to its one caller. The options were (a) type the public `GameState.match` as `MatchState` and give the `MatchRecord` only to `DirectorContext` and `Simulation`, (b) an ESLint rule that refuses `.match.<writer>(` outside `match/`, `Simulation.ts` and tests, or (c) leave it to review. The owner chose "(a) Read-only type". So: the compiler, not review, stops other callers. Scheduled: its own PR from the phase branch after the match-director PR merges, together with the HC-S7 split of `Simulation.ts`; not built in the match-director PR. | Architecture 03 §2 (`GameState` and `MatchRecord` sketch) and 12 §4, when built; `INBOX.md` HC-Q46 |

The owner's words, from the playtest dialog (2026-11-04):

> if the core starts at 1, we can do 1->50. everything else is fine. the display should start at 1
> and max at 50. the design is probably already where it needs to be; my wording may have made you
> overcomplicate it

> when weapons are added, note that the click shouldnt only attack when a target is there. a
> phyisical weapon (e.g., sword or axe) swipes the area in front of the user (range depending on
> weapon's reach). a projectile weapon (e.g., an arrow shot from a bow or a projectile shot from a
> staff) simply goes in the direction it was cast/launched. Whatever is in the area the weapon's
> range reaches, is hit by the weapon and takes damage. if a weapon can sweep (e.g., an axe sweeps
> an area of X in front of the character in an arc), whatever iscontacted by the weapon takes
> damage. this would mean that if a minion and an object are within that sweep, they would both
> take damage.

> heal over time. slow % based healing flat across all minions and legends. some classes get better
> healing (tunable) but for MVP, flat rate. heal triggers after 10 seconds out of combat. healing
> increased by 10% while under a friendly tower that isnt under attack.

The owner's other answers were choices from the dialog: "Keep all three" (click rules), "Keep all" (minimap
defaults), "E2: reach the walls" plus the chase-hold fix, "F1: 300 XP to every Legend on the
destroying team" with "no XP" for buildings, "Legends + minions" (minimap units), "Keep (1)
approach from the facing side; change (2): chase an enemy inside the tower" (bot siege), and
"Camp mobs count too" (minion XP, amends DEC-165).

**Defaults taken on DEC-188 (default, owner accepted 2026-11-05).**

1. "Out of combat" means the unit dealt and took no damage for 10 s. Any damage counts, also 0.
2. "Under a friendly tower" first meant the tower aura radius of 12 m (`balancing.md` "Auras"). The owner's zone answer above replaces it: the zone is inside the walls.
3. "Not under attack" means the tower took no damage in the last 10 s.
4. The bonus does not stack.

## Batch 2026-11-04, round 2 (the owner's calls on the match director's critic round 2, `feat/match-director`)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-04 on two findings of the match director's pre-PR critic, round 2,
and on a lint failure in the main checkout (`INBOX.md` HC-Q43 to HC-Q45), asked in chat. The
stored answers are kept verbatim outside the repository, in
`eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-11-04-match-director-round-2.md`. The
answers to HC-Q43 and HC-Q44 overrule the agent's recommendation; HC-Q45 had none.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Docs changed                                                                                                                                       |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-177 | **`GameState.match` is a `MatchRecord`, which holds the match writers** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q43; changes where DEC-172's writers live). Asked: `GameState.ts` had grown to 349 lines, over the ~300-line guide, mostly from the writers of DEC-172. The options were to add a `MatchRecord` store in `state/` in this PR, or to schedule the split with HC-S7. The owner chose "A: Build MatchRecord now" (the recommendation was to schedule it). So: `state/MatchRecord.ts` implements `MatchState` and holds its five writers (`setPhase`, `holdIdle`, `endMatch`, `seatHuman`, `advanceTimeline`); `GameState.match` is a read-only `MatchRecord`, and every caller writes through `state.match`. | Architecture 02 §2 (`state/` tree line and row), 03 §2 (`GameState` and `MatchRecord` sketch), 12 `paths` and §4 (writer names); `INBOX.md` HC-Q43 |
| DEC-178 | **`@eternalus/core` exports `opponentOf`** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q44; DEC-171). Asked: the client's `game/session/buildMatchSetup.ts` kept its own one-line copy of the "other team" rule that DEC-171 put in core's `teams/`. The options were to export `opponentOf` from core and use it in the client, or to leave the copy. The owner chose "A: Export opponentOf" (the recommendation was to leave it). So: `packages/core/src/index.ts` exports `opponentOf`, `index.test.ts` pins it, and `buildMatchSetup.ts` uses it.                                                                                                                                                                         | None (public API: `packages/core/src/index.ts`); `INBOX.md` HC-Q44                                                                                 |
| DEC-179 | **ESLint and git ignore `eternalus-agent-work/`** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q45; ADR 0014). Asked: in the main checkout, `npm run check:quick` failed on untracked probe tests that agent sessions left in `eternalus-agent-work/`. The options were to add the folder to ESLint's `globalIgnores` in this PR, or to leave the lint config and have the sessions that own the probes move them out of the repository. The owner chose "A: Ignore eternalus-agent-work/". So: `eslint.config.js` lists the folder in `globalIgnores`, and `.gitignore` lists it too, because ADR 0014 allows only untracked folders in the ignore list.                                                                      | None (`eslint.config.js`, `.gitignore`); `INBOX.md` HC-Q45                                                                                         |

## Batch 2026-11-04 (the owner's calls on the match director's critic round 1, `feat/match-director`)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-04 on the decision page of the match director's pre-PR critic,
round 1 (`INBOX.md` HC-Q35 to HC-Q41). The stored answers are kept verbatim outside the repository,
in `eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-11-04-match-director-calls.md`. DEC-160
to DEC-169 are reserved for the minion stack, so this batch starts at DEC-170.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Docs changed                                                                                                                                         |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-170 | **The countdown's end tick has one formula, `countdownTicks()` in `config/`** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q36; DEC-139). Asked: `Math.round(countdownSeconds × tickRate)` was computed in `defaultGameConfig.ts`, `validateGameConfig.ts`, `SpawnSystem.ts` and `GuardRoster.ts`, and a validation rule exists only to keep the timeline's countdown-over mark and the first-wave gate on the same tick. The options were one helper, a helper plus an at-countdown-end timeline mark kind, or no change. The owner chose "One helper". So: `config/countdownTicks.ts` holds the formula, and every production copy and test uses it. That includes `ai/LeashBehaviour.ts`, whose unrounded copy now rounds like `SpawnSystem` (the same tick at the default 10 s), so `ai/` may import `config/`. The helper takes `countdownSeconds` and `tickRate`, not a `GameConfig` as the option said, because the default config and the validator compute the tick before a whole config exists. The validation rule stays.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Architecture 02 §2 (`config/` line, `ai/` row); `packages/core/AGENTS.md` (`ai/` row); `INBOX.md` HC-Q36                                             |
| DEC-171 | **The team helpers live in `teams/`** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q37; DEC-139). Asked: the `'light'` / `'dark'` pair and the "other team" rule repeated in five or more files, and `teams/` held types only. The options were to build it in this PR, to log it for its own PR, or to drop it. The owner chose "Build in this PR". So: `teams/playableTeams.ts` holds `PLAYABLE_TEAMS` (Light first, the order of every per-team loop and hash), `isPlayableTeam()` and `opponentOf()`. They replace the repeats in `ObjectiveLostCondition`, `validateGameConfig`, `EnragedSystem`, `MinionStrengthStep`, `Simulation`, `GameState`, `SpawnSystem`, `LaneStructures`, `AllyDamageStore`, `StateHasher` and `BotLeaves`. Team mappings that include `neutral`, map geometry and palettes keep their literals. `teams/` still imports nothing; `config/` and `ai/` may now import its values.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Architecture 02 §2 (`teams/` line and row, `config/` and `ai/` rows); `packages/core/AGENTS.md` (`config/`, `teams/`, `ai/` rows); `INBOX.md` HC-Q37 |
| DEC-172 | **`GameState.match` is read-only; four narrow writers change it** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q38; DEC-139; extends DEC-84 (6)). Asked: any system, command handler or bot controller could set `match.result`, which turns off every command, or `isIdleHeld`. The options were to build it in this PR, to log it for later, or to drop it. The owner chose "Build in this PR". So: every `MatchState` field is `readonly`. Beside `setPhase`, only these write it: `holdIdle` (the countdown phase), `endMatch` (the `MatchDirector`, once; a second call throws), `seatHuman` (`Simulation`, once, when the match is built; a second Legend throws) and `advanceTimeline` (the `MatchDirector`, once per fired timeline entry).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Architecture 03 §2 (`GameState` sketch), 12 §4 (`MatchState` sketch); `INBOX.md` HC-Q38                                                              |
| DEC-173 | **The human's Legend is the roster's `human` slot** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q35; DEC-139; changes plan A19). Asked: the critic found that the human's Legend still spawned at a point the client sent. The options were to keep the seat as its own `MatchConfig` entry and spawn it at its team's Stronghold through the code the bots use (A1, the accepted plan), or to make the player a slot of the recorded roster (A2). The owner chose A1, with the note "We should always target the most future-proof solution that incurs the least possible tech debt and carries the most optimal architectural design. A critique should be run to check if this is as good as or better than the planned architecture, and accept it only if it is." The architecture critic found A2 better: the client's match setup already lists the player in its roster, the replay setup records the roster, and DISC-51 makes the human their team's Rover. Asked again in chat, the owner chose "Keep A2" and confirmed its defaults. So: a `RosterSlot` has `pilot: 'bot'` (with its `difficulty`) or `pilot: 'human'`, and `MatchConfig.seat` is gone. A roster has at most one human slot, on `config.match.playerTeam`; a second one, or one off `playerTeam`, throws. A human slot whose class does not exist or is not a Legend logs ERROR `core.match` and the match plays on without a human, because the class comes from saved data and can be stale (ADR 0023); a bad bot slot throws. The refused slot still counts toward `legendsPerTeam` and the Unit cap. The client gives the human the Rover role, so it spawns at its Stronghold with the other Rovers, in roster order. The client's `botRosterSlots` is now `rosterSlots`, and `ai/` may import `config/` (DEC-170). | Architecture 11 §7, 12 §4 and §13, 18 §8, 03 §4 and §9, 15 §7, 02 §2 and §12, 08 §11 note; plan A19 and §4.5 (amendment note); `INBOX.md` HC-Q35     |

## Batch 2026-11-04 (the owner's ruling on PR #69's review, `feat/buildings-and-map-objects`)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-04, given in chat on the review of PR #69 (finding B1) and on its
pre-PR gate critic (better-than-design item B1). The answers are kept verbatim outside the
repository, in
`eternalus-agent-work/pr-review-2026-10-29/pr69/owner-answers-2026-11-04-pr69.md`.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Docs changed                           |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- |
| DEC-158 | **The Stronghold wall is a shield arc from cliff face to cliff face, not a ring** (answers PR #69's review, B1). Asked: PR #69 made the default wall an open, enemy-facing shield arc whose rear is closed by mountain cliffs and the map edge (per side: walls 9 → 6, turrets 12 → 10, gates unchanged); its only source was the author's annotated image, and architecture 06 said the circular ring stays. The owner kept the arc: "the wall shouldnt be a circle but rather an arc that stretches from cliff-face to cliff-face. ... there is no reason a stronghold engineer would create a wall that can be circumvented and attacked from behind if the citadel already were built in an alcove of a mountain side". So: the default layout's `wallRing.frontArc` (`content/maps/defaultMapLayout.ts`) is the design, and the wall carries the shield art. Each arc tip meets its cliff corner, which seals the rear; a test keeps them together. The requirement "wall around the Stronghold" is met by the arc and the cliffs together. ADR 0009's "wall rings with gates at lane exits and turrets at vertices" still holds: the ring is open at the rear. | architecture 06 (amendment 2026-11-03) |
| DEC-159 | **The closed-ring wall is removed; every Stronghold wall is the shield arc** (answers the PR #69 pre-PR gate critic, B1). Asked: after DEC-158 the code still kept the closed circular ring as an option (`wallRing.frontArc` optional) that no map used; it added branches in 9 files and made the shield art guess each wall's vertex order. The owner chose "Remove it": `wallRing.frontArc` is required (`content/maps/maps.types.ts`), the closed-ring branches and the tests that covered only the closed ring are removed, and the tests of behaviour the arc keeps (gate order, chord width, arc splitting, gate narrowing, lane and tower anchors) run on the arc.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | architecture 06 (amendment 2026-11-03) |

| DEC-156 | **The replay's command log stamps each external command with the tick it applied in** (answers PR #68's review, follow-up 2; amends ADR 0025 §4). Asked: `Simulation.enqueueCommand` logged a command with the current tick, but a command enqueued during `step()` (from an event handler) applies in the next step, so a replay applied it one tick early; nothing enqueues during a step today. The options were a guard that throws, logging at apply time, or no change. The owner chose "Log at apply time (Recommended)". So: `step()` logs each external command (player, developer menu) when it applies it. The chosen option said one line moves; built that way, it also logged the bot commands, which share the queue, so external and bot commands now wait in two queues (forced by the choice; see the answers file); `step()` applies the bots' first, then the external ones. A replay feeds the external commands before each step, after the bots queued theirs, so this is the order it reproduces, also for an external command enqueued during a step before `AiSystem` runs. Bot commands stay out of the log. Both tests in `Simulation.replay.test.ts` fail on the old code. | ADR 0025 (amendment 2026-11-03); architecture 03 §3 |
| DEC-157 | **PR #68 targets `phase/3-sprint-3-work` and merges now, with a merge commit** (answers PR #68's review, follow-up 1; HC-Q21 (b) is met). Asked: the PR targeted `phase/2-sprint-2-work`, while the open minion PRs and the phase PR to `main` (#65) target `phase/3-sprint-3-work` (both branches on the same commit). The owner chose "Retarget phase/3". A first question said HC-Q21 (b) also covered #56, #57 and #58; it names nine PRs, all merged, so it was asked again, and the owner chose "Merge now". So: #68 merges into `phase/3-sprint-3-work` once its CI passes; #56, #57 and #58 resolve the conflicts with it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `INBOX.md` HC-Q21                                   |

(2) Critic loop 3 was the third and last loop, and its fix (the critic's BD-A, `5500a0c`) changed structure. The owner selected "Allow attack": "A plain click on the world attacks even with the explorer shown; only drags and roof clicks are claimed." ## Batch 2026-11-02 (PR #64 fix: capture zones, sholtzman)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-02 on the PR #64 review page (capture points as zones, by
Aryan976503) and on its follow-up questions. We finish the PR for its author and push to his
branch. DEC-144 is the minion work's; DEC-147 to DEC-149 are the HUD branch's.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Docs changed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-145 | **A capture zone covers its building's floor; Battlefield 6 is the model (PR #64 review, Q1 to Q3).** The owner's words: "if the capturable point is not circular, it should not be a circle. you go inside the captureable area and capture from there"; "the cathedral's capture point should fill the cathedral's interior to the walls, not just the central area. if an enemy legend is hiding in a corner behind objects, the other team will need to find and defeat that legend, or simply have more of a presence"; "as we have done weighed captures (e.g., legends hold a heavier "weight" than light host units), we may simply want to show density% for each team"; "we should look into how BF6 actually does this and emulate their implementation of the capture feature"; "we have an additional checkpoint that allows neutral units from camps to capture a point, which BF6 does not do. our design is unique in this way and should remain the case"; Q2 and Q3: "A (Recommended)"; "we will handle these fixes, not Aryan. we push to his branch." (a) **Area:** a zone covers the floor of the building its map feature stands for: an outer capture point the court inside its fort's walls, the Cathedral its whole floor to the walls (nave, transept with both chapels, apse). A unit counts when its centre is on that floor. This replaces the PR #49 review's "Zones are circles (F8)" and 07 §7's zone radius. (b) **`checkpoint-reached`:** fires when a side's push takes a capture past checkpoint 1, 2 or 3, in order; never on a drain or a revert. `capture-progress` stays for the progress bar. (c) **Density:** each side's weighted presence inside a zone is published, and the client shows each team's share. Where the numbers show, the owner chose "A (Recommended): HUD, while your Legend stands in a zone", built in the HUD PR; PR #64 publishes the per-side weights and draws a thin split bar (Light, neutral, Dark shares) under each zone's progress bar. (d) **BF6 as the model:** progress follows the advantage, equal presence pauses it, an owned zone drains to neutral before it fills (DEC-86 (c), (d), (f) already do this); the zone shows as an outline on the ground and a minimap marker; each side's presence in the zone is shown. (e) **Kept, not BF6:** neutral camp units capture (DEC-86 (g)). (f) **Revert unchanged:** asked whether a reached checkpoint holds when the zone empties, the owner chose "A: only full capture locks"; partial progress reverts to the last locking state (DEC-86 (h)). (g) **Each checkpoint fires once per side until the zone changes owner:** the owner chose "Once per side until owner changes". A side that wins back its own drained zone fires nothing, so a zone cannot be farmed for the assist mobs a checkpoint spawns (assist mobs give XP, game doc 04). | `architecture/07-structures-obstacles-and-destructibles.md` §7, §11, §12; `architecture/04-entity-architecture.md`; `architecture/03-game-model.md` §4; `requirements/06-mvp-requirements.md` FR-031. The zone areas live in `map/zoneArea.ts` and `content/maps/buildingGeometry.ts` |
| DEC-146 | **PR #64's capture timings are `TUNE` placeholders.** The owner's words in the posted PR #49 review (2026-10-30): "Tuning: your values are fine as `TUNE` placeholders, placed in `GameConfig` (F7)." The values: 12 s from neutral to full at a margin of 1, 20 s to revert, a margin cap of 3.
