## Batch 2026-11-02 (the owner's answers on HC-Q30 and HC-T1 and two process rulings, `feat/hud-plan-b5`)

The owner's answers of 2026-11-02, given on the decision page https://claude.ai/artifact/KfoL8MV45nJndNmDV3dND6 and in chat. His words are kept verbatim outside the repository, in
`eternalus-agent-work/hud-charselect/decisions/owner-answers-2026-11-02-hc-q30-hc-t1.md`. DEC-138 to DEC-143 were reserved for this batch on 2026-11-02. The first row was renumbered from DEC-138 to DEC-154 the same day, before this branch was pushed: SamarjitBhogal's guard-post ruling had already pushed DEC-138 (#56).

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Affects                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-154 | **Docs that are wrong about code the critic and reviewer team verified are corrected without asking the owner** (process ruling; amends OD-26 in `15-figma-frames-and-creative-decisions.md`). Asked nothing: it is the first of two process rulings the owner gave with his HC-Q30 and HC-T1 answers. He wrote: "we must ensure that critic cycle(s) are run on these to ensure they are within the architecture, as good-as the architecture, or better than the existing architecture. documentation decisions that are based on current state of code shouldnt need approval. documents should simply be changed to reflect the code if the code has been veriefied and run against a critic and reviewer team. this only applies to me, but no reason to ask if we can change the documentation to be accurate rather than inaccurate. (e.g., function is named doTing, but dos call it doStuff; current;y youve been asking me if changing the documents to say that the function is called doThing is ok since the documents are wrng. this is tedious and unnecessary since i would have approved the change which is already in the docs by this time. this is only for me and our internal reviews since i still need to check design decisions. but this shouldnt be such a tedious process. if the docs are wrong and the code is right, the docs must change to reflect the code.)" So: a doc that disagrees with code that has passed the critic and the reviewer team is changed to say what the code does, with no question to the owner; this holds for the owner's own docs too. Design stays his call: a change to what a doc intends, not to what the code is called or does, still waits for his answer. Every edit to his docs is reported to him. Applied first in `da57c29` (T-202 and T-221 redrafted to the built crash flow before they were applied). | `AGENTS.md` "Documentation"; OD-26; `INBOX.md` HC-T1                                                                                                                |
| DEC-139 | **Each critic loop also judges whether each decision, in docs and in code, is the right one, and whether a better design exists** (process ruling; extends the pre-PR gate's critic step, ADR 0010). Asked nothing: the second process ruling of the same answer. He wrote: "The critic loop must not only verify the validty of the claims, but also that these are the correct decisions to be made, not only in documentation, but in code, and that there was not a better way to move forward with the code decisions made to improve the architecture or make implementation better, more SOLID, m,ore in-line with OOP best practices, and DRY (if not already the case)." So: the critic's brief now includes that question; a finding that a design more SOLID, more in line with OOP best practice or more DRY exists is a better-than-design item (tell the owner in chat, add it to `INBOX.md`; once signed off, update the design docs in the same PR), not a violation. Written into root `AGENTS.md` "Before opening a PR" step 1 and into the `architecture-reviewer` subagent's instructions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `AGENTS.md` "Before opening a PR"; `.claude/agents/architecture-reviewer.md`; ADR 0010                                                                              |
| DEC-140 | **The crash popup's saving text and note-not-saved text are the owner's words; the saved text, the second click and the empty screen stay as built** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q30 items 1, 2, 3, 4 and 6). Asked "1. The text while the note saves. Recommendation: \"Saving the note...\".", the owner wrote: "Change or ask \| Saving the note and report...". Item 2, "The report was saved as <file>.": "Approve". Item 3, the text when the report is saved but its note is not: "Change or ask \| The report was saved, but the note was not. Copy the note and send it to the team via the shared Discord channel." Item 4, the saved text and the second click on "Return to menu": "Option A". Item 6, a crash of the home screen itself leaves the empty screen with no "Reload" button: "Option A". So: `crashReportPopup.saving` is "Saving the note and report..." and `noteNotSavedMessage` is the owner's text. "Saving the note and report..." is accurate: the note route reads the saved report, writes it again with the note in it and renames it over the old file (`apps/client/errorReportsPlugin.ts`, `addNote`). The "Copy report" button of a failed note write is unchanged. Built in `62652b0`; each value cites DEC-140.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `apps/client/src/ui/strings.ts`, `ui/screens/CrashReportNotice.tsx`, `game/session/CrashReportFlow.ts`; ADR 0025 §5; DEC-106, DEC-124; `INBOX.md` HC-Q30            |
| DEC-141 | **A screen crash during the pre-launch reveal returns to the menu, for a crash only** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-Q30 item 5, a design change to the session's list of allowed phase changes). Asked "5. A screen crash during the pre-launch reveal. ... Options: (A) keep this; (B) add the move from pre-launch to the menu for a crash only (a design change to that list) and return to the menu with \"The screen stopped because of an error.\". Recommendation: B", the owner wrote: "Option B". So: `SESSION_TRANSITIONS` gains `pre-launch` to `menu`; `handleScreenCrash` already returned to the menu with `'screen-crashed'` wherever that move exists, so the reveal now does too, and both popup buttons reach the menu from there. The reveal still has no cancel: `PreLaunchState` handles no intent, so no player intent takes the move (a test pins it). Built in `62652b0`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `apps/client/src/game/SessionController.constants.ts`, `game/session/handleScreenCrash.ts`; architecture 13 §2; reference 14 (the reveal); ADR 0025 §5; DEC-124 (b) |
| DEC-142 | **All the HC-T1 rows are approved, except T-199** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-T1, the text of the design-doc edits). Asked to approve the 54 rows left on the decision page, the owner wrote: "every group: Approve (all 54 rows approved), except" T-199. So: 52 rows are applied in `da57c29`, in 20 files; T-159 changes its status only (its table waits for the HC-Q18 (2) build); T-125 edits the minion architecture (18), which SamarjitBhogal owns, so it is flagged for him in the PR changelog. Each touched doc has `last-reviewed: 2026-11-02`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | The docs the rows name (list: `INBOX.md` HC-T1, Round B27); decisions DEC-124, DEC-125, DEC-154                                                                     |
| DEC-143 | **T-199 changed: the minimap is player HUD and always drawn; developer tools show only with the developer tools; a roof hides on its own** (answers `INBOX.md` HC-T1 row T-199, architecture 06 §10 "Free view and interior"). The owner wrote: "Change or ask \| the minimap should always be shown in the new stylized HUD. he dev options may not always be shown (such as free view, etc), but the minimap is not a dev item and is part of the HUD for players. building roofs should automatically dissapear when players enter said buildings, not require players to hide them." So: the minimap is part of the players' HUD and is always drawn; the developer tools (free view, the overview, the explorer toolbar and PR #30's click-to-toggle-roof) show only when the developer tools are shown; a building's roof hides on its own while the player's Legend is inside it, with no key or click. ## Batch 2026-11-02 (#56: fight to the death, sholtzman)

The owner's request and answers to the four calls (A to D) of the "Fight to the Death" decision
page and the three calls (E to G) of its round-2 page, given on 2026-11-02 on PR #56 (minion M2c,
`feat/minion-m2c-objectives-guards`). The round-2 page came from the critic's first review of the
build; the guard answers came in chat after its second review. DEC-138 to DEC-143 are taken by
other branches. The owner's words are quoted below the table.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Where                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-144 | **Distance, then time: hordes fight to the death (MS-36).** Opposing hordes walked through each other after a short fight: after a kill a mob returned to its slot, left every enemy alone for a cooldown, and the horde marched on. Built from the owner's seven answers: (A) a mob fights a target with no time limit while the target is inside its leash, a Legend too; when the target leaves the leash the mob takes the best-ranked enemy in reach (priority, then distance); (E) with none, it keeps chasing and attacking the target for `leashGraceTicks` (3 s) and fights on if the target comes back inside; after that the leash breaks and the mob returns to its formation and marches on; (B) any enemy in reach is fought, and when a target dies the mob takes the next enemy in reach at once, with no cooldown; a returning mob fights too; (C) guards follow the same rules inside the circle their guard plan draws around the structure, with no grace (E): they never take or keep a target outside it, take the next enemy in it, and walk back to the post when the target leaves, so an enemy that follows is fought nearer the structure; a deliberate step back to pull the target toward the tower is not built (the owner: emergent is enough); guards have no rest after a break, and the guards of one structure defend each other: an enemy attacking any of them anywhere in the circle is in reach of all; a guard fighting at the circle's edge can stand beyond its target, so `guard-within-leash` allows one decision's walk plus that stance; leaving the circle a little to fight a Legend that dances at the edge waits for tower fire (`INBOX.md`); (D) "call for help": an enemy attacking a horde-mate within `callForHelpRadius` is in reach; the radius equals the leash radius, not the whole horde, because a mob may not take an enemy outside its leash; (F) after a leash break the mob leaves alone, for `aggroCooldownTicks`, only the enemy that escaped, Legend or minion, and never while that enemy attacks the mob or a horde-mate; (G) the `returning` state now acts as the resting state; it stays, and `INBOX.md` asks the minion owner whether to keep it, merge it or give it a job. **Supersedes DEC-135's "A Legend still escapes after 2.3 s"**: the aggro duration is gone. | `architecture/18-minion-system.md` MS-36, §6.1, §7; `architecture/11-game-ai.md` §4.3; `architecture/15-diagnostics-and-runtime-testing.md` §3; the rule lives in `ai/LeashBehaviour.ts`, the numbers in `content/ai/aggro/` and `content/structures/guardPlans.ts`; `INBOX.md` (G, edge dance) |

The owner's request (2026-11-02, as recorded by the session that received it; "..." marks a cut
it made): "armies often walk through each other after a short fight. the units should never move
on if enemies are nearby. they should be fighting to the death. if a minion is running away, and
is being chased. if they get cornered, they should turn and fight. but currently the leashing is
broken. we often see dark and light minions piling up at each other's gates. this shouldn't be
able to happen if both teams have equal numbers of minions spawning ... this needs to be fixed,
if minions are our responsibility."

The owner's answers (2026-11-02):

- **Call A**, option b (recommended): "It turns and fights as soon as it is back inside its
  leash. As long as the minion is fighting, the leash remains. the leash has a max distance, so if
  the enemy (e.g., a legend) runs out of the leash, the minion leash breaks after N time (e.g., 3
  seconds) and it leashes to the closest enemy (if any) or returns to its formation and proceeds
  down-lane." Approved.
- **Call B**, option a (recommended): "It fights any other enemy in reach. similarly to the answer
  from above, a minion attacks anyone in its range. if a legend is fighting a minion/horde then
  runs away, the leash remains until the legend gets outside its distance, not determined by time.
  if a legend remains in its distance longer than the 3 second leash, the leash remains. if the
  legend or minion the minion is attacking leaves its distance, this is when the leash deprecates
  over 3 seconds. again, distance, then time." Approved.
- **Call C**, option a (recommended): "same rules as the rest. distance (within the guard's
  distance), then break the leash when the enemy is outside its distance. the tower guards try to
  remain at the tower, so if the enemy leaves the tower and stays in its range, then the tower
  guard must either fight or return to the tower. it cannot be persuaded to run far away from its
  post. they take the next enemy within their range, not run back to the tower and wait before
  coming back out again. if another enemy is nearby, fight them. if this pulls them farther from
  the tower, they try to pull their target toward the tower. as the tower can defend itself with
  projectiles, the tower guard will take advantage of this and pull its enemy inwards." Approved.
- **Call D**, option b: "call for help". Approved.

The owner's answers to the round-2 page (2026-11-02). Each call quotes the question and the option
the owner chose, which read as shown:

- **Guard pull**, asked in chat before the page (should a guard step back toward its tower to
  pull its target in, once towers shoot?): "B: No, emergent is enough".
- **Call E**, "The target leaves the leash, and no other enemy is in reach. What does the minion do
  in the 3 s grace": option b (recommended), "It keeps chasing and attacking for up to 3 s. If the
  target comes back inside, the fight goes on. After 3 s the leash breaks and it walks back to its
  formation. Guards get no grace: they stop at the edge of their circle at once, because they
  "cannot be persuaded to run far away from [their] post"."
- **Call F**, "In the 3 s rest after a leash break, whom does a minion leave alone?": option b
  (recommended), "Leave alone only the enemy that got away, for 3 s, unless it attacks the minion
  or a horde-mate. Every other enemy in reach, Legend or not, is fought."
- **Call G**, "The "returning" state now acts the same as the rest state. Remove it?": option a
  (recommended), "Keep it in #56. Add an INBOX item so that Samarjit decides."

The owner's guard answers, asked in chat after the critic's second review (2026-11-02):

- **Guard rest**, asked with the options "No rest for guards", "Keep the 3 s rest" and "Guards
  defend each other": "no rest for guards AND they defend each other? maybe the defend happens if
  the dancing at the edge thing happens, wherein the defender is more aggressive and willing to
  leave the circle a little. if it cant attack the legend (they keep dancing), the guards return to
  their post. thoughts?" Asked back, the owner chose "1 + 2, skip 3 (Recommended)", which read:
  "No rest, and guards defend each other. Edge dancing waits for tower fire; an INBOX item keeps
  the idea. Small code change plus tests, then the critic re-checks it."
- **Guard leash check**, "a guard fighting an enemy at the edge of its circle can stand up to
  about 3 m beyond it (body radii + melee reach), and the leash check (8 m + 2 m tolerance) fails
  at 10.01 m ... How should it be fixed?": "Widen tolerance (Recommended)", which read: "No
  gameplay change. The check's tolerance becomes 'one decision's walk + the farthest a guard
  stands from a target in reach': 2 m becomes 5 m (1.2 walk + 3.1 heavy-vs-heavy stance +
  margin)."

## Batch 2026-10-30 (game-loop plan, GL-5 beyond the plan, GL-4 review calls, FR-007 points)

The owner's answers, in chat on 2026-10-30, to the GL-5 items beyond the plan and to the GL-4
pre-PR architecture review. Verbatim with the questions as put:
`eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-10-28.md`, last section. DEC-128 is taken
(PR #63), so these are DEC-129 to DEC-131. The owner's fourth answer (how scoring keeps recent
damagers went to a design critic first; DEC-132 records its verdict.

## Batch 2026-10-30 (game-loop plan, the GL-4 counters and structure damage)

The owner's answers, in chat on 2026-10-30, to the GL-4 report's question A (which of 12 §5's
per-Legend counters GL-4 keeps) and to the GL-4 correctness critic's finding 2 (what the structure
damage column counts). Verbatim with the questions as put:
`eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-10-28.md`, last two sections. DEC-117 to
DEC-119 stay with the HUD lane, which numbers in that gap; DEC-120 and DEC-121 are taken, so these
are DEC-122 and DEC-123.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Docs changed                                                                                                               |
| ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-122 | **The per-Legend stats hold only the counters that built work fills.** GL-4's `LegendStats` holds `kills`, `deaths`, `assists`, `minionKills`, `neutralKills`, `structureDamage` and `score`. Each later task adds its counter (for example `finalLevel`) with the code that fills it, so the results never show a false zero. This applies the rule of DEC-113 (10) to the counters and amends the game-loop plan §4.4 ("The other 12 §5 counters stay in the type"). Owner, asked to keep only the filled counters or add all now as 0: "A1: filled counters only (Recommended)". | Built in `feat/match-scoring` (`state/ScoringState.types.ts`); the game-loop plan §4.4 cites it                            |
| DEC-123 | **The structure damage column counts all damage a Legend deals to an enemy structure, shield bars included.** A hit that a BuffTower's shield bar absorbs counts as structure damage, as a hit on a Legend that only its shield takes already counts toward an assist. Owner, asked to count health and shield or health only: "Health + shield (Recommended)".                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Built in `feat/match-scoring` (`match/Scoring.ts`, `state/ScoringState.types.ts`); architecture 12 §5 `damage-applied` row |

## Batch 2026-10-30 (game-loop plan, the GL-1 and GL-2 doc sign-off)

The owner's answer, in chat on 2026-10-30, to the pre-PR architecture review's major finding
(`eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/arch-review-gl2-round-1.md`, M1 and its D table): design
text changed beyond the plan's accepted rows. Verbatim with the question as put:
`eternalus-agent-work/game-loop-plan/owner-answers-2026-10-28.md`, last section. DEC-93 was
taken by the PR-review session the same afternoon, and DEC-94 to DEC-112 by other work, so this
is DEC-113.

| ID      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Docs changed                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-113 | **The GL-1 and GL-2 design-doc changes beyond the plan are signed off.** (1) `match/` may import `components/` and the `diagnostics/` logger types. (2) `config/` may import `teams/` types. (3) `DirectorContext` is `{ state, enterPhase }`; an action gets content values through its constructor. (4) `MatchDirector` takes one options object. (5) The director, not `MatchDirectorSystem`, does nothing once the result is set. (6) `match/defaultMatchDirector.ts` builds the default director. (7) A phase entered on tick T takes effect from step T+1, so a 10 s countdown holds 301 steps. (8) The match fields join the state hash only once one leaves its initial value, as shields and heading already do. (9) `Snapshot.match` carries `humanLegendId`. (10) `MatchResult` gains `legends` and `teams` with scoring (GL-4), and `timeline` and `achievements` with the work that fills them; this amends the plan's GL-2 "Done when" and §4.5. ## Batch 2026-10-30 (game-loop plan, after the match ends)

It is not a freeze-frame like the countdown (DEC-88). The client stops the loop 5 s later. Owner, choosing between "(a) Freeze-frame: `victory` and `defeat` hold every unit idle, the same way the countdown does" and (b): "(b) Keep running: the world runs on until the client stops the loop a few seconds later, which is what the match director doc recommends now." Owner: "nothing during the countdown. lets just have everyone in idle position with idling animations once those exist. 10 seconds, no movement. you can turn your character around to look around. nothing else." and "allow turning. act as if all units are idle, not as if game is paused." Amends DEC-84 (1) ("only walking is held; attacks and casts work"). | Built in `feat/match-director`: `CountdownPhase` sets `MatchState.isIdleHeld`, and `MovementSystem` and `CombatSystem` skip while it is set. The owner's answers of 2026-10-30, given to the pickup session as one dialog of three questions: where the developer camera hotkey is handled (the critic's item m1), whether a chord with modifiers is a one-shot press (item B1, a better-than-design item) and the hotkey on each operating system (item n1). His words are kept verbatim outside the repository, in
`eternalus-agent-work/hud-charselect/decisions/owner-answers-2026-10-30-b1-n1-m1.md`. DEC-117 and DEC-118 were free on every local and remote branch, in every local worktree and in the agents' working folders on 2026-10-30 (the game-loop session left 117 to 119 to this lane); DEC-119 stays unused.

| ID      | Decision | ------- | | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-117 | **A key chord with modifiers is a one-shot press, and the client-only one-shot actions run from `MatchHost`'s key listener** (answers the critic's items B1 and m1). Asked whether a modifier chord such as Ctrl+Shift+F3 is a one-shot press, matched on the key press with exactly the chord's modifiers and never held input, and whether that rule goes into architecture 13 §6.1 and §6.2, the owner chose "Adopt it (Recommended)". Asked where `toggle-developer-camera` is handled (a raw window key listener in `MatchHost` today), he wrote: "no tech-debt. if moving m1 is better, do it." So: a one-shot action is matched on the key press with exactly its chord's modifiers and is never held; `MatchHost` runs it from a table of action to handler (`runOneShotAction`, one entry today: `toggle-developer-camera`, which shows or hides the map explorer of `CanvasPointerControls`), and a new one-shot action is one entry in that table. `Match`'s `clientActions` stays the path for the client edges of state `Match` owns, such as the camera zoom; it is still not built. The recorder's notes, not the owner's words (the orchestrator decided m1 on his delegation, and gave these reasons): (1) the snapshot of the tick path carries key codes only, with no modifiers, so a modifier chord cannot resolve there; (2) the explorer belongs to `MatchHost`'s pointer controls, not to `Match`; (3) `Match`'s loop stops while the match is paused, the reason architecture 13 §6.2 already gives for the pause key. The wording for architecture 13 §6.1, §6.2 and §9 and for architecture 17's recipe R21 waits in `INBOX.md` HC-T1 (T-196, T-209 to T-212). Recorded from `eternalus-agent-work/hud-charselect/decisions/owner-answers-2026-10-30-b1-n1-m1.md` (outside the repository), questions 1 and 2. | `apps/client/src/game/MatchHost.ts` (`runOneShotAction`, `oneShotActions`); `game/input/InputMapper.ts` (`isActionKey`); `bridge/chordKey.ts`; architecture 13 §6.1, §6.2, §9; architecture 17 R21; DEC-105; `INBOX.md` HC-B3, HC-Q29, HC-T1                                                                                                                |
| DEC-118 | **Both developer camera chords stay bound on every platform, and the player's own platform's chord is listed first** (answers the critic's item n1; amends DEC-105 in the order only). Asked which chord each operating system gets (both chords bound everywhere with the Settings screen listing Ctrl first, as built; keep as is; or one chord per platform), the owner chose "C: both, Mac chord first on Mac". So: Cmd+Shift+F3 and Ctrl+Shift+F3 are both bound on every platform; on an Apple device (user agent matching Mac, iPhone or iPad) Cmd+Shift+F3 comes first, and elsewhere Ctrl+Shift+F3 does. The order is set once, when `keybindings.ts` loads. The recorder's note: DEC-105 says the hotkey is Cmd+Shift+F3 on a Mac and Ctrl+Shift+F3 on Windows and Linux; the build already bound both on every platform, so this answer settles the order and keeps both. Recorded from `eternalus-agent-work/hud-charselect/decisions/owner-answers-2026-10-30-b1-n1-m1.md` (outside the repository), question 3. Note 2026-10-30 (the critic's loop 2, items V1 and N1; the decision is unchanged): the order is no longer set when `keybindings.ts` loads, because `game/input/` holds bindings as data and no platform reads. `main.tsx` reads the platform (a user agent that contains "Mac"; iPhone and iPad user agents contain "like Mac OS X") and gives it to `SettingsController`, which lists the platform's own chord first each time it publishes the settings (`ownPlatformChordsFirst` in `game/input/bindingEdits.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `apps/client/src/game/input/keybindings.ts` (`toggle-developer-camera`); `apps/client/src/game/settings/SettingsController.ts` (`publish`); `apps/client/src/game/input/bindingEdits.ts` (`ownPlatformChordsFirst`); `apps/client/src/game/isAppleUserAgent.ts`; the Settings screen's chord list; DEC-105; architecture 13 §6.1 (T-196); `INBOX.md` HC-Q28 |

A test file that needs a page (real key events, the focus) opts in with `// @vitest-environment happy-dom` on its first line; every other test stays in Node. The recorder chose happy-dom over jsdom as the lighter of the two. Performance, measured on 2026-10-30: the shipped bundle is unchanged (a development dependency is never bundled; the build's content-hashed file names and sizes are identical), and the client suite's run time stayed within its run-to-run spread (5.7 s before, 5.0 s after). | `apps/client/package.json`; `ui/screens/ClassSelectStep.viewDetails.test.tsx`; `apps/client/AGENTS.md` "Tests"; architecture 09 §9 and 13 §8; requirements `05-engineering-standards.md` (the client test row) |
| DEC-112 | **A mouse button that closes View Details does not step the browser's history.** Told that a `pause-menu` bound to a side mouse button closes View Details on its press, so the press's release reached the browser after the view's listeners were gone and went back one page, he answered: "guard it, please." So when a mouse-bound `pause-menu` press closes the view, that press's release (its `mouseup`, then the `auxclick` that ends it) still loses its default; the guard ends at that `auxclick` or at the next press. The recorder's note: side buttons that no action is bound to keep the browser's default on every screen; he was not asked about those.                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `ui/components/modalKeyFilter.ts` (`preventReleaseDefault`)                                                                                                                                                    |

| DEC-109 | **View Details opens a details screen on every layout and step; selecting is a separate button** (amends DEC-52's default and CD-10; answers the rest of `INBOX.md` HC-Q5). Asked whether the alternate class layout's "View Details" button, which selected the shown class, should open the new View Details screen with a separate Select Class button beside it, he answered: "View Details on class shows the class details. View Details on the subclass shows the subclass details (e.g., abilities and lore). Similar screens for both. View Details to open screen. Close screen. Select class. View Details to see Legend details. Close screen. Select subclass. Lock in from selection screen." So: on both class layouts and on the Legend step, View Details (key V, DEC-103) only opens that step's details screen (the class's on the class step, the Legend's abilities and lore paragraph on the Legend step) and never selects; Escape closes it; the class is confirmed with Select Class and the Legend with LOCK IN on the selection screen. The alternate layout (62:70) therefore shows "V View Details", opening the same class details screen as the primary layout, and gets the primary layout's Select Class button. | DEC-85 | **Mirrored random camps are accepted for PR #30, corner camps included (#30-A).** The owner's words: "Accept the mirrored randomness for this PR. The "sides differ" is an improvement for the next sprint (stretch goal) or post-mvp. Randomness is good; mirrored randomness is fine for now." For the MVP, the match seed moves each forest camp to a random spot inside its slot and shifts the corner camps at random. The two sides stay mirrored (ADR 0009). DEC-15's per-side placement ("the sides differ") and the proposed ADR 0017 become a next-sprint stretch goal or post-MVP; ADR 0017 stays a draft. This answers DEC-15's open corner-camp question for the MVP. PR #30 must add the rule to the map doc as a dated amendment, not a rewrite (its round-2 review, B2).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `INBOX.md` (DEC-15 item, question 1). With PR #30: `architecture/06-map-and-minimap.md` (dated amendment)                                                                                                                            |
| DEC-86 | **Capture-point rules (#49-A, #49-B and two follow-ups).** The owner's words: "A (All units)"; "Having majority in the zone (more Legends (worth 10x minions), more minions, etc) causes your team to begin capture"; "the capture point slowly reverts to its last locking state"; "option 1 (Tie = no change) but neutral units can revert the point to neutral. player-owned structures should never be able to be inside the capture zone but if somehow one was, it does not count. projectiles also do not count."; and, asked which holds, "Neutral can re-take". (a) **Who counts:** every unit inside the zone: Legends, minions and neutral-team units. Structures and projectiles never count. (b) **Weighted majority:** a side's presence is the sum of its units' weights; a Legend weighs 10 minions (`TUNE`). The side with the highest presence moves the progress toward itself. (c) **Tie:** when the two highest presences are equal, nothing changes. (d) **Rate:** it grows with the margin over the next side, up to a `TUNE` cap. (e) **Locking states:** neutral, fully Light and fully Dark. A zone stays in one until a capture changes it, so an owned zone with nobody inside stays owned. (f) **Taking an owned zone:** the owner's progress drains to 0 first, then the taker's fills; the zone stays the owner's until the capture completes. (g) **Neutral re-take:** neutral units with the majority drain the owner's progress to 0, and the zone becomes neutral again. This replaces #49-B's "Neutral only at the start before first capture". (h) **Revert:** with nobody inside, a capture that has not reached a locking state slowly reverts to the last locking state (`TUNE` rate). (d) and (h) use the wording of the posted PR #49 review (its rules 4 and 8). The owner's answers do not spell them out. The mini-boss lock (FR-008) does not change. Replaces FR-031's "captured by uncontested allied presence". | `requirements/06-mvp-requirements.md` FR-031; `architecture/07-structures-obstacles-and-destructibles.md` §7                                                                                                                         |
| DEC-87 | **The spawn timeline (final version of 2026-10-30).** The owner's words: "Tier-1 spawn towers should spawn units at 2 minutes post-0-tick so they arrive at middle as reinforcements for the opening host, rather than getting to the middle fight first"; "move the tier-2 tower's first wave spawn up to 2.5 minutes post-0-tick"; "for heavy spawns, we can change their spawn to occur at 3mins post-0-tick"; "1 minute cooldown on 2nd-tier towers (offset by .5 minutes from 1st tier towers), 1.5 minute cooldown on citadel host group spawn". (a) **Clocks** start when the countdown ends (the owner's "tick 0"; simulation tick 300 for the 10 s countdown at 30 ticks/s). (b) Each living tier-1 **SpawnerTower** sends a light horde down its lane at +120 s, then every 60 s (first time amended to +165 s by DEC-93). (c) Each living tier-2 **BuffTower** sends a medium horde down its lane at +150 s, then every 60 s, 30 s after the tier-1 waves (first time amended to +195 s by DEC-93). (d) The **Citadel** (`Stronghold` in code) sends a heavy horde at +180 s, then every 90 s, down the team's most-struggling lane (DEC-89). (e) **Opening Hosts:** one per lane at the lane base by the Citadel gates, there from load-in and idle until the countdown ends; what the countdown freezes is DEC-88. (f) A destroyed tower stops spawning (per tower). (g) Horde sizes 16 / 8 / 4 and the cap of 144 units per team stay for the MVP: "for now. would like to increase and adjust in the future but concerns about browser-based performance for class MVP." Supersedes DISC-10's cadences and first-wave timing (batch 2026-10-03), the owner's earlier answers of the same day (#38-C's 240 s, the 180 s first medium horde, waves counted from load-in) and FR-005's "every 60 s after the countdown". Answers INBOX's minion M1 question (1).                                                                                     | `requirements/06-mvp-requirements.md` FR-005. Not changed here (other owners): FR-003 (countdown length, game-loop work), `architecture/07` §6, `architecture/12` §2, `architecture/18` §3.1, `content/structures/spawnWaves.ts`     |
| DEC-89 | **The most-struggling lane** (where the Citadel's heavy horde goes). The owner's words: "down most-struggling lane (wherever the towers have taken the most damage, or enemy is pushed up the most, or is where most allies have died or been harmed)"; asked whether "allies" means Legends, minions or both: "both". For each team and lane, three signals: (1) the team's own towers on the lane took the most damage; (2) the enemy pushed furthest along the lane; (3) the most allies died or got hurt there. "Allies" means Legends and minions. The weights start equal (`TUNE`, `content/ai/laneAssessmentWeights.ts`). These replace the three signals that architecture 18 §3.3 settled on 2026-10-15 (structure damage not dealt, structures lost, pushed back) and answer the terms and weights of MS-9; the tie-break is not part of the ruling. Doc 18 §3.3 shares one lane assessment between the heavy horde and the corner-camp reward wave, so FR-033 follows. That is a consequence of the design, not part of the ruling.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `requirements/06-mvp-requirements.md` FR-005, FR-033. Not changed here: `architecture/18` §3.3 and §10, `state/LaneAssessment.ts`                                                                                                    |
| DEC-90 | **Legend XP curve target.** The owner's words: "Legends shouldnt exect to hit level 50 until about 20 minutes into the game if they are actively farming minions, hitting neutral camps, and getting kills/assists on Legends. Levels should take more XP to level up, increasing per-level (e.g., 1-2 == 100 xp, 2-3 == 200xp, 3-4 = 400 xp, 4-5 == 750 xp, etc; numbers as example not as stone-set records)." A Legend that actively farms minions and camps and gets kills and assists on Legends reaches level 50 at about 20 minutes. The XP to the next level rises with each level. The 100 / 200 / 400 / 750 XP steps are examples of the shape, not values. The curve stays `TUNE` content (`content/progression/levelCurves.ts`), sized to this target. The owner set it after the spawn-timeline page estimated Legends at level 25–36 before the first heavy fight ("we will need to do some scaling on this").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `requirements/06-mvp-requirements.md` FR-049                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| DEC-91 | **Horde event names (#38-E, answer A).** The owner's words: "A but ensure the HTML gives a breakdown of exactly what this means for Host spawning and game flow. Is it suggesting that host units no longer spawn from towers? I am confused as to what it is doing." It does not: towers still spawn Hosts. Only the event names in the requirement text change, to follow the event list. Each spawned unit emits `entity-spawned` and each horde `horde-spawned`; a horde with no members left is removed with `horde-dissolved`, which has no cause field.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `requirements/06-mvp-requirements.md` FR-037                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| DEC-93 | **Tier-1 and tier-2 first waves move later (amends DEC-87 (b) and (c)).** DEC-87 gives the intent of the tier-1 time in the owner's words: "so they arrive at middle as reinforcements for the opening host, rather than getting to the middle fight first". A probe of the #38 fix (seed 1, 3 lanes) then found that lights sent at +120 s reach the lane centre before the opening Host on every lane: about 7–9 s early on the middle lane and 38–40 s early on the outer lanes. Asked whether the number or the intent wins, the owner chose "Intent: tier-1 2:45, tier-2 3:15". Timed from the countdown's end, each living tier-1 **SpawnerTower** sends its first light horde at +165 s, then every 60 s. Each living tier-2 **BuffTower** sends its first medium horde at +195 s, then every 60 s, still 30 s after the tier-1 waves. The Citadel's heavy horde stays at +180 s, then every 90 s, so it now leaves before the first medium horde. The probe put the earliest first time that lets the lights follow the Host at about 127 s (middle lane) and 160 s (outer lanes), so +165 s leaves about 5 s on the outer lanes (`TUNE`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `requirements/06-mvp-requirements.md` FR-005. Not changed here (other owners): `content/structures/spawnWaves.ts` (the #38 fix)                                                                                                      |
| DEC-94 | **Object pooling covers projectiles, not event payloads (PR #51, SCRUM-427).** Asked whether high-frequency `GameEvents` payloads should come from the new `ObjectPool`, the owner delegated the call: "Whatever is the best architecturally, performance wise, best design-wise, SOLID, DRY". Chosen: pool projectiles (and particles) only; event payloads stay ordinary objects. `GameEvents.emit` keeps every payload in the recent-events ring buffer, and handlers may keep a payload after `emit` returns, so a recycled payload would silently change those records; pooling payloads safely needs deep copies of nested payloads (`DamageSource`, `memberIds`), which is more code for a cost nobody has measured. The game model doc already says high-frequency events "may be pooled (Recommendation, profile first)", and the entity architecture's pooling section names only `ProjectilePool`. Payloads join the pool only if the NFR-023 soak profile shows GC pauses from them.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `requirements/02-coding-principles.md` (Object Pool row), `reference/glossary.md` (Object Pool). PR #51: `ObjectPool.ts` TSDoc, phase 1 plan sprint 1.2 item, INBOX                                                                  |
| DEC-99 | **Hard control and `control-immune` (PR #52, SCRUM-228).** (a) `crushed` is hard control, so `control-immune` refuses it. The owner's choice: "Yes, hard control". The `control-immune` immunity list in the abilities proposals now reads stun, knockup, pulled, taunted and crushed. Derived from that document's rule "`control-immune` is present for the tick after any hard control expires" (not asked separately): `control-immune` also follows a `crushed` that runs out, as it follows `stun`. (b) Re-applying a `stun` that is still active restarts its timer (`refresh` stacking), and `control-immune` follows only when the stun runs out. The owner's choice: "Allow refresh" ("Keep as built: each new stun resets the timer"). The declined alternative was that an active hard status cannot be refreshed; so a producer that re-applies a stun before it ends can hold a target until it stops.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `reference/suggestions/more-abilities.md` §7 (`control-immune` row). PR #52: `architecture/05-abilities-weapons-and-loadouts.md` §8 status note, `content/status-effects/crushed.ts` and `stun.ts`, INBOX status effects (6) and (7) |

Record that on the PR, and apply my answers below as amendments." and added: "yes i suggested it be added and it run the pr-review skill to reduce the amount of edits i need to make in pr reviews, as it is the same skill we use. what must be noted though is that taste and design decisions are still for me to make, so they must be given to me." and "As the product owner, i need to have visibility and input on design, arch, and taste decisions." The owner's picks on follow-up questions the same day: "Block (Recommended)": CodeRabbit posts each such decision that the base-branch docs do not already make as a blocker that mentions the product owner, and its check fails until the owner signs it off or a teammate overrides it; "Player-facing + arch (Recommended)": the rule covers anything a player sees or feels (in-game names, UI, visuals, gameplay rules and numbers) and architecture and design-doc changes, not code identifiers; "No, visibility only (Recommended)": no owner-only CODEOWNERS entry, so a teammate can still approve. | `decisions/0018-coderabbit-runs-the-pr-review-skill.md` (amendment item 1); `.coderabbit.yaml` (review instructions, custom check)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

It never approves. Turn on Code Owner review for phase/\*\*, so GitHub, not a YAML file, requires a person. [...]" with "yes". On the bot accepting the author's own text as a sign-off: "absolutely not. this will let anyone accept a pr." For the owner's own PRs, the pick on a follow-up question the same day: "Yes, mine counts (Recommended)". So a sign-off counts only from the person who gives it: their own review or comment on the PR, their own description on a PR they wrote, or an accepted ADR or decisions-log row already on the base branch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `decisions/0018-coderabbit-runs-the-pr-review-skill.md` (amendment items 2 and 3); `.coderabbit.yaml`; `process/sdlc.md` (Reviews, Change control); `process/git-workflow.md` (Pull-request rules); `process/guardrails.md` (CodeRabbit row, Manual setup); `agents/tool-setup.md` (CodeRabbit); `agents/checklists.md` (AI-PR review); `.github/pull_request_template.md` |

| DEC-98 | **Learnings: this repo only, an admin approves each new chat learning, and every learning is logged in `docs/` for the product owner.** The owner picked "This repo only, and an admin approves each learning. Delete the stored "no human approval" rule [...]" and added: "this, yes, but learnings should be forwarded to me for review. Samarjit is a trusted developer, but some learnings may require my insight. I should at least be notified of learnings and they should be reviewable/revertible. | DEC-84 | **The GL-1 critic points are settled; the opening Hosts wait at their lanes; the countdown is 10 s.** (1) During the countdown only walking is held; attacks and casts work (GL-1 as built). (4) The tower-wave start stays with the minion code ("this should be in PR 38"): `SpawnSystem` gates tower waves on the countdown length, and the game model doc no longer lists `SpawnSystem` as a `phase-changed` listener. The opening Citadel Host groups spawn at match start, one per lane, at the base of their lane. They stand beside the player's Legend during the countdown and march down their lanes when it ends (owner: "During the intro 10 second movement hold, the player can see the host groups on either side of them"). (5) The countdown is 10 s, not 60 s (amends DEC-81; the agent's reading of "the intro 10 second movement hold"). (6) The four GL-1 changes that the architecture critic rated better than the match-director doc are signed off: a missing phase or action handler fails at compile time; validation and the director use one formula for the tick an entry fires; only `GameState.setPhase` changes the phase; the first phase starts before tick 0 with no `phase-changed` event, so the freeze covers tick 0. | `docs/plan/game-loop-and-results-plan.md` §6. Pending: FR-003, FR-005, FR-007; architecture 03 §4, 12 §1 to §4 (with GL-1) |

Crashes ask for a note and save the report even with no note; silent errors (failed loads, broken saved data, runtime-check failures) are reported without asking. ## Batch 2026-10-28 (game-loop plan, GL-Q answers)

The owner's answers to `docs/plan/game-loop-and-results-plan.md` §5b, given in chat on 2026-10-28
to the session continuing that plan. Quoted in plan §5c; paraphrased here. He asked that they be
recorded "so i do not need to make these decisions twice". DEC-71 was the first number free on
every local and remote branch and every worktree on 2026-10-28 (the HUD branch's worktree holds
DEC-57 to DEC-70).

| ID     | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Docs changed                                                                                                                                                |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DEC-71 | **The sovereign (King) is the only win; the Stronghold is a container (game-loop plan GL-Q2).** A team loses when its sovereign dies. The Stronghold (Citadel) holds the sovereign and its throne room and is the spawn point for its Host. It is no longer a win condition or a listed objective. Its walls can be destroyed, one segment at a time where damage is focused. Replaces FR-002 and FR-021's "Stronghold destroyed".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `docs/plan/game-loop-and-results-plan.md` §5c; ADR 0021 (accepted 2026-10-29, DEC-83). Pending: FR-002, FR-021; architecture 12 §1, §6; 07 §1, §8 (plan §6) |
| DEC-72 | **What the sovereign is (GL-Q1, GL-Q3 (c)).** A team unit that fights, with a move set that is small for the MVP and can grow. It stays in a throne-room zone, middle-back in the Citadel (owner: "think Erebor Throne Room"; PR #30's hall once it merges). A guard Host of light, medium and heavy units protects it. It is zone-locked, but it attacks from long range when attackers stay out of its reach and keep hitting it. It attacks neutrals that enter the Citadel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `docs/plan/game-loop-and-results-plan.md` §5c; ADR 0021. Pending: architecture 11 §4 (controller), 04 §1 (family)                                           |
| DEC-73 | **When the sovereign can be hurt (GL-Q3 (a)).** Walls always exist. The sovereign takes no damage while any BuffTower of its team lives; all of them must fall first. Every attacker deals full damage. Its swings clear whole minion waves (cleave). Until BuffTower entities exist, nothing makes it immune. The owner said "fine as long as a dev handles this before the 27th" of the plan's earlier "no human can win in the browser"; the plan reads that as covering this gap too, so BuffTower immunity must exist before the 2026-11-27 presentation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `docs/plan/game-loop-and-results-plan.md` §5c; ADR 0021. Pending: FR-022, FR-029                                                                            |
| DEC-74 | **Sovereign name, stats and regeneration (GL-Q3 (b)).** The display name is content per team, "Emperor" by default, and can be changed. Code names are generic, so they work for modes with other win objectives: `ObjectiveLostCondition`, `WinObjective`, family `sovereign`, leaf `Monarch` (the agent's pick; the owner accepted them on 2026-10-29, DEC-83). Stats are `TUNE`, starting from the mini-boss anchor in `balancing.md`. After 90 s out of combat it regenerates slowly (rate `TUNE`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `docs/plan/game-loop-and-results-plan.md` §5c; ADR 0021                                                                                                     |

## Batch 2026-10-28 (the owner's non-blocking answers, `feat/hud-and-character-select`)

The owner's answers of 2026-10-28 to the 42 non-blocking questions of
`feat/hud-and-character-select`, given in chat the same day on a second decision page (the PR
register, `docs/changelog/pr-feat-hud-and-character-select.md`, lists all 42 in its section "Owner
answers of 2026-10-28 (non-blocking)"). His instruction with them: "Make sure all of these items are
written down. Don't want to have to write this all again." His words are quoted under each item he
answered in `INBOX.md`; the rows below record the game-design decisions among them, quoting him
(obvious typos corrected), and mark the recorder's readings as such. His rename (DEC-58) overrides
every name in his other answers of the day: where he uses an old name, the new one is meant. Doc
approvals (`INBOX.md` HC-T1), kept defaults and the choices he left to the architecture are
recorded in `INBOX.md`; his answer on FR-111 is recorded in ADR 0022. DEC-58 to DEC-70 were free on
every local and remote branch, in every local worktree and in `INBOX.md` on 2026-10-28.
